## US-3854 — New Landing page-Login

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-3854 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | chandrasen.ingawale |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2023-07-25T08:12:40.08Z |
| Created Date | 2023-02-28T12:59:29.157Z |
| Tags | removed |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/3854) |

### Description

As an HEIC Portal user*, I want to navigate to the new landing page using my existing credential from the legacy portal so that I can access functionalities corresponding to my roles

### Acceptance Criteria

~~Post successfully login from the legacy authentication page, I should be able to successfully redirect to the latest landing page and view all the required features.~~
**_Pre-requisite:_**
HEIC portal users: Marketing(Design), PMG, Planning, WE,Manufacturing  

**
QA, QC, NDE(Non Destrictive Examination),Auxiliary, Logistics
  
**
**_  
_**
**_Functional:_**
  *  User should be able to successfully redirect to the latest landing page, provided the entered credentials are correct. 
  * Once the user gets successfully login to the portal, then the user should be able to see below mentioned feature. 


  1.  L&T Heavy Engineering Logo
  2.  Mega Menu button 
  3. Smart Factory (Static content) 
  4. Project Details 
  5. Reference Documents 
     1. Specification 
     2. Shop DIN 
     3. CRS DIN 
     4. DCR 
  6. Reference Standards
     1. ASME Sec. 8 
     2. ASME Sec. 9 
  7. Quality  

     1. NCR 
     2. CTQ
  8. Planning  

     1. Concerto
     2. Digi-Eye
  9. Project Documents  

  10. Search Bar (at the top)  

  11. Notification "Bell" Icon  

  12. Avatar 


  * After clicking on the L&T Heavy Engineering logo, user should redirect to the landing screen. 
  * If the user is already viewing the landing page, then it will get refreshed. 
  * If the user is present on some other page, then the user should get redirected to the landing page. 
  * On the new landing page, an "I" button should be visible on the header. 
  * Once the user clicks on this "I" button, the user should gets redirected to the legacy portal landing screen in a new tab without the need to enter credentials if the session is already active 


  

**_Non Functional Requirement:_**  

The performance, speed and device compatibility should be the same as per current structure  

  

Note: Refer attached document for detailed information.

### Description

As a ENGG3/PMG3/PLNG3/WE3/QI3/QA3/NDE3/AUX3/LOG3 user, I want to enter Procedure document data as per assigned procedure for the specific project, so that accurate details can be captured in the document

### Acceptance Criteria

**Perquisite:**
Design Department must have Documents assigned and tagged to specific project against the department.  

  

**Functional:**  

  1. User should be able to expand a procedure by clicking the arrow as per visual: [L3_Maintain Procedure (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/89e9f384-19ce-4ae8-9942-15680814350f-dcba/screen/f439ee13-451c-4234-9427-467e95b0678d)  

  2. User should be able to view an editable line item with Type as Procedure  

  3. Using O365 editable option  

     1. User should be able to view the Procedure document automatically fetched from the Procedure Master against the line item  

     2. User should be able to view the Procedure document via O365 in an overlay format where the user can also view and edit as well as save it on the go  

  4. Please note that user will not be able to delete the line item containing "Procedure" as Type  

  5. User should be able to re-sequence the documents by dragging them  



**Usability:**
The design should be compatible with Laptop, Tablets (iPad and Android) chrome browsers. 
  
Technical: Please refer attached document for technical specifications

## US-9006 — US -3 Success while Fetching Part/H-BOM/Seam list data automatically

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-9006 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\Common-Feature Team 3 |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2023-12-25T05:38:06.807Z |
| Created Date | 2023-05-11T12:07:11.33Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/9006) |

### Description

As a DE3/DE2 User, I want to Maintain 3D automation configuration to get data from PLM light to the IEMQS automatically, so that in future I will not have to fetch data manually every time from PLM light to IEMQS.

### Acceptance Criteria

### Prerequisites -  

  * Users should be logged in.   

  * 3D automation configuration should be done by Key user.


### Functional -  

  * System should start fetching selected data as per "Business Process" field.  

    1. System should fetch "Part List" data through middleware,  

       * System should fetched only approve status part list data.  

       * System should add the fetched part list data as per their respective location in same as AS-IS process.
    2. System should fetch "H-BOM List" data through middleware,  

       * System should fetched only approve status H-BOM list data.  

       * System should add the H-BOM list data as per their respective location in same as AS-IS process.  

       * System should insert Bill of material items in equipment as per attached image.
         * Item code - Project No.-Equipment, (Refer 2nd Image of H-BOM).
         * Item code - Project No.-CRS, (Refer 3rd Image of H-BOM).  

    3. System should fetch "Seam List" data through middleware,  

       * System should fetched only approve status Seam list data.  

       * System should add the Seam list data as per their respective location in same as AS-IS process.
    4. System should not allow Users to Modify/ Edit/ Delete/ Insert in all the fetched Part List/H-BOM List/Seam List in their respective sessions for this configurable project. 
    5. System should show approved status for all the fetched Part List/H-BOM List/Seam List automatically in their respective sessions in AS-IS process. 
  * User should be able to see Successful Message in Notification at the landing page "X Part/X- BOM/X-Seam lists fetched successfully." every time data flow from PLM to IEMQS  

  * System should get the data from BRAH-M platform to IEMQS for the configurable projects only. 
  * If any Part List/H-BOM List/Seam revised, 
    * System should fetch only approved revised Part List/H-BOM List/Seam List data from BRAH-M to IEMQS through middleware.
    * System should not add duplicate data in their respective location.


  

**Special Note**  

  1. The existing tables webpages stored procedures functions views to be used in the to be processed to get the data automatically. 
  2. System should allow to upload data into the IEMQS for which configuration is not done. 




### Usability:  

The design should be compatible with Laptop, Tablets (iPad and Android) chrome browsers.
  

Notes
  1. Project related Part page in Legacy system.  
  
  

  2. Project related H-BOM data page in Legacy system.  
\--->\--->  
  

  3. Project related Seam data page in Legacy system.

### Description

As a DE3/DE2 User, I want to Maintain 3D automation configuration to get data from PLM light to the IEMQS automatically, so that in future I will not have to fetch data manually every time from PLM light to IEMQS.

### Acceptance Criteria

Prerequisites -  

  * Users should be logged in. 
  * Navigation: Latest Landing Screen -> Mega Menu-> Configure 3D Automation 


  

**Functional -**  

  1. System should capture the PS number/ login ID of person who initiates the request.
  2. If System capture error while fetching Part List/H-BOM List/Seam List data
     * System should show Error message in landing page. 
       * Message - "Error while fetching data, please download Error log file here." 
       * Notification also contains link of file. 
       * Generated file directly downloads on the local drive.
         * Name of downloaded file: PSNo._DATE_TIME 
         * DATE format (DDMMYYY) 
         * TIME format (HRMINSEC)
         * example: 20001746_04-04-2023-041847  

     * System should generate and send error file by mail to the person who initiate the request as per PS number/ login ID. 
     * System should send same error log file by mail to IT administrator (User who are having IT1, IT2 and IT3 roles) 


  

**Usability-**
  * The design should be compatible with Laptop, Tablets (iPad and Android) chrome browsers.

### Functional -  

  

User should be able to view "Configure 3D Automation" Grid with list of added configure project.  

  * User should be able to view the grid as per visuals [Configure 3D Automation Prototype Link](https://xd.adobe.com/view/26f6ec1f-d057-4865-9074-8b20608fcefe-73a5/)
    1. Initially, till a single data added by the user in the Grid, 
       * System should show blank grid shown as per visuals.
    2. User should be able to view "Product Type, "Business Unit", "Contract Number", "Project Number" and "Business Process" column as per visual.
       * System should automatically fetch previously added data.
    3. User should be able to view "3D automation toggle" button of "Yes/No for every row as per visual.
       * System should freeze the toggle button to "Yes" shown as per visual.
          * System should not allow user to toggle to "No" button, 
    4. User should be view "Created By" and "Created On" column as per visual.
       1. System should capture the "Name (PS Number)" in the "Created By" as per "PS Number" of login person.
          * Format -"Name (PS Number)"
       2. System should capture the date at which data added in the Grid at "Created On" field shown as per visual.
          * Date Format - DD/MM/YYYY
    5. User should be able to view pagination and Sorting of grid shown as per visual. 
    6. Upon Clicking Filter button, user should be able to see Two fields.  

       * Through "Search" field, 
         * User should be able to search grid data and also able to clear the filer.
      * Through "Creation Date" field, 
        * User should be able to search by date of Creation ("Created On" column).



Usability:  

The design should be compatible with Laptop, Tablets (iPad and Android) chrome browsers.

### Description

As a DE3/DE2/DE1 User, I want to Maintain 3D automation configuration to get data from PLM light to the IEMQS automatically, so that in future I will not have to fetch data manually every time from PLM light to IEMQS.

### Acceptance Criteria

### Prerequisites -  

  * Users should be logged in.
  * 3D automation configuration should be done by the Key user.


### Functional -
  

  * System should stop listing the projects in FKMS (Full Kit Management System) in AS-IS Process.
    * System should check for the project whose 3D automation started in the "Configure 3D Automation" **for  ****Part List/H-BOM Lists.**  (Can check in Grid).
       * System stops listing the projects in FKMS at "Project drop down menu "for those whose 3D automation started for Part/H-BOM lists. 
  * System should check for the project whose 3D automation started in the "Configure 3D Automation" **for  ****Part List/H-BOM Lists.**  (Can check in Grid).  

    1. System should disable "Prepare Part/BOM with excel" option in action column at existing AS-IS IEMQS process only for DE3, DE2 and DE1 users (Refer attached image 1.)  

    2. System should disable "Create Part/BOM data" option in action column at existing AS-IS IEMQS process only for DE3, DE2 and DE1 users (Refer attached image 2).  

  * System should check for the project whose 3D automation started in the "Configure 3D Automation" **for  ****Seam lists.**  (Can check in Grid).  

    * In Maintain Seam List page of existing AS-IS IEMQS process, 
      * Upon clicking ADD NEW, system should not select/show configured project in "Project" dropdown menu.
      *  (Refer attached image 3).



**Special notes -   **
>   * System should allow AS-IS process in FMKS functionality for the projects in the IEMQS for which configuration is not done. 
>

## US-9315 — US -1 Configure 3D Automation-Main Screen

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-9315 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\Common-Feature Team 3 |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2023-12-25T05:38:06.807Z |
| Created Date | 2023-05-23T11:38:24.777Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/9315) |

### Description

As a DE3/DE2 User, I want to Maintain 3D automation configuration to get data from PLM light to the IEMQS automatically, so that in future I will not have to fetch data manually every time from PLM light to IEMQS.

### Acceptance Criteria

## Prerequisites:
  * Users should be logged in. 


## Navigation:
## 
  *  Latest Landing Screen ----> Mega Menu----> Configure 3D Automation.


 
### Functional -  

  

Upon clicking " Configure 3D Automation " button in Mega Menu,   

  1. User should be able to view "Configure 3D Automation" page shown as per visuals - [Configure 3D Automation Prototype Link](https://xd.adobe.com/view/26f6ec1f-d057-4865-9074-8b20608fcefe-73a5/)  

  2. User should be able to view the hierarchy of the page via breadcrumbs with back button shown as per visual.  

  3. User should be able to view **" Product Type"**, **" Business Unit"**, **" Contract Number"** and **" Project Number" **fields as per visuals.  

     * User should be able to view single select drop-down menu with predictive search functionality.  

     * All respective fields are mandatory.  

     * Product Type - Location?  

     * Business unit - Location?
     * "Contract Number"- Location?
     * "Project Number" - Location?
     * If user select any one Project Number in multiselect whose Quality project does not exist,  

       * System should disable the checkbox option of "Seam list" in the "Business Process" multiselect dropdown.
  4. User should be able to view "Automation" toggle button to toggle between to "Yes" and "No" shown as per visual.  

     * Initially, system should select/toggle to "No" field as shown as per visual,  

       * Upon toggling to "Yes" in "Automation",
         * System should disable "Product Type", "Business Unit", "Contract Number" and "Project Number" fields with selected data.
         * In backend, System should check "Quality Project" is available for all selected "Project No" or not,  

         * System should enable the Business Process field.
       * Upon toggling to "No" in "Automation",  

         * System should enable "Product Type", "Business Unit", "Contract Number" and "Project Number" fields with selected data.  

         * System should disable the Business Process field.
  5. **If user select first Project number directly** , System should fetch "Product Type" "Business Unit" and "Contract Number" automatically,
     * System should disable the "Product Type" "Business Unit" and "Contract Number" fields.
     * System should populate/fetch data automatically only if "Product Type" "Business Unit" and "Contract Number" are blank initially.
  6. User should be able to view **" Business Process"** fields shown as per visual.  

     * User should be able to view Multi select drop-down menu with predictive search functionality.  

     * All options in dropdown are static-
        1. Part List
        2. H-BOM List
        3. Seam List 
           * System should disable the Seam List option, 
             * If "Quality Project" is not available for selected "Project No".  

           * System should enable the Seam List option, 
             * If "Quality Project" is available for selected "Project No".
        4. Mandatory to select at least one option,  

  7. User should be able to view "Save" button to be shown as per visual.  

     * System should enable" Save" button only when all the mandatory fields are filled.
     * Upon clicking Save button,  

       * **Flow 1 -** If "Project No." is already existing for selected "Business Process", 
         * System should provide a pop-up window "Record already exists for selected Business Process. Please refer below table." with "OK" button.
         * Upon clicking on OK button,
           * System should clear the "Business process" field.
       * **Flow 2 -  **Else, system should show a "Confirmation" pop-up window with "Yes" and "No" buttons.
         * Message in pop up window - Are you sure you want to start 3D automation?
         * Upon clicking "Yes",
           * System should start fetching data of selected "Business Process" through middleware.  

           * System should add multiple rows of all selected combination "Configure 3D Automation" grid.
            * System should capture "PS Number" of person as per login details.
            * System should capture "Date" when project added in the list.


 Usability-
  * The design should be compatible with Laptop, Tablets (iPad and Android) chrome browsers.

**Notes -  
**
  * Mapping for Project with Quality project.

## US-10269 — TECH:: Validation on TC No, WBMS & Rev No combination existence

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-10269 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | s.gayathri4 |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\NLP Team |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2023-11-06T15:49:15.64Z |
| Created Date | 2023-06-09T11:46:29.443Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/10269) |

### Description

Validate the combination of TC No, WBMS & Rev No existence  
If exists, display error message  
if not, ERP API to be called.

### Acceptance Criteria

## ​Prerequisites -  

## 
  * Users should be logged in.
  * Navigation: Latest Landing Screen-> Mega Menu-> TC review-Weld Consumable


### Functional -
  1. User should be able to view to add new Test Certificate page by clicking on "+Add TC" as per visual: [TC Review (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/f2f11170-dac5-4337-ad6d-c032a8445f84-a7a2/screen/773480a1-5e70-427d-b424-cce7d558df16?fullscreen)
  2. User should be able to view the hierarchy of the page via breadcrumbs as per visuals.  

  3. User should be able to view progress bar dynamically in all pages "Upload test Certificate->Data extraction in Progress->Ready for review->Approved" as per progress as per visual. 
  4. User should be able to view and enter "TC Number" manually. 
  5. User should be able to view "WBMS" fields as per visual.  

     * User should be able to view and enter data manually. 
     * WBMS format \- HXX-XX-XX 
     * System should check that combination of TC number; WBMS number and Rev No should always be unique.   

       * In case this combination exists then system should show message that TC No has been reviewed for this WBMS No and Rev No.
  6. User should be able to view "Rev No." and enter data manually as per visual. 
  7. Upon clicking "Get Data" button, system automatically fetch WBMS No data from ERP LN.
     * For Invalid WBMS No and Rev No, System should pop up a message "This data not exist." ( which Handled by middleware team.


  

**Special Notes-  **
  1. The data should be fetched from ERP LN through Middleware to IEMQS. 
  2. Table structures, table schema, mandatory, primary index, the complete details need to be taken for ERP LN 


**  
**
**For Table details, please refer attached documents.**  

  1. Please keep the table name as it is.  

  2. Supplier No and TC No field length and data type to be taken from ERP LN  

  3. Supplier NO, TC NO, and Status fields needs to be added in LTWPS056.  



  

Usability:
The design should be compatible with Laptop, Tablets (iPad and Android) chrome browsers.

## US-10530 — Access Request - App Hub

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-10530 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Samir shah |
| Area Path | ECOM |
| Iteration Path | ECOM |
| Changed Date | 2024-08-01T06:30:03.98Z |
| Created Date | 2023-06-17T09:55:20.153Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/10530) |

### Description

Access request is an common application for Domain ID Creation, VPN Access and Internet Access of any newly joined employees and trainee.  

  

**The following are step to generate access request in Application Hub.**
  

  1. Once log in into the Access Request, they have the option to add new request 
  2. Upon clicking the add new button, the user will be redirected to a page which will ask the following detail. 
     1. Location 
     2. Option (What type of request) 
     3. Purpose 
     4. Username 
     5. Reporting to (whom are you reporting to) 
     6. Mobile number 
     7. Valid date 
  3. Upon entering all the details, the user will have the option to save or submit the request.
  4. Once the user submits the request, they can view their request on the Access Request List. 
  5. On Submission of request, one email trigger to approver to review the request. 
  6. Once a request is added, the Approver has the access to either Approve, Postpone or Reject the request. 
  7. The Approver has to write a remark as in why are they rejecting the request otherwise the website shows an error. 
  8. If the approver postpones or rejects a request, it shows on the Pending List. 
  9. Once the Approver approves the request, the details of the request is mailed to the Infra team. 
  10. The Infra team then works on providing the user with the access to what was asked via the request. 


  

**Application Integration approach changed.**
  

Earlier, employee details were getting fetched from Infor ERP LN database using the link server, due this tightly coupled integration, if Infor ERP LN database service goes down then access request application will also get down. To avoid such incident we have used a new approach to create masters in ECOM database and used across ECOM applications. Using the database scheduler data will synchronized from Infor ERP LN database to ECOM database on daily basis.

### Acceptance Criteria

Functional requirements:  

  1. User should be able to submit request 
  2. Approver should get email notification
  3. Approver should able to take any one of the action like Approve, Reject or postpone.
  4. User from Infra team gets email notification for required action.

## US-13509 — Mega Menu -3D automation

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-13509 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2024-10-18T07:10:49.83Z |
| Created Date | 2023-08-09T08:09:04.427Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/13509) |

### Description

As a DE3/DE2 User, I want to Maintain 3D automation configuration to get data from PLM light to the IEMQS automatically, so that in future I will not fetch data manually every time from PLM light to IEMQS.

### Acceptance Criteria

**Pre-requisite:  
**
  * User should be able to logged in the system. 


**Navigation:  
**
  * As a DE3/DE2 User \-->Mega Menu---> Click "Configure 3D Automation". Page. 


  

**Functional:  
**
**  
**
Feature | Function | Module | Sub Process | Locations | Roles   
---|---|---|---|---|---  
3427 | Design | BRAH-M Integration  |  "Configure 3D Automation"  | HZW,PEW,RNW | DE3 and DE2 User   
  

  * In Mega Menu, this module needs to be shown as per above mentioned flow.
  * For Quick links, 
    * User can add links   "Configure 3D Automation" from Mega menu.


**Usability:**
  * The design should be compatible with Laptop, Tablets (iPad and Android) chrome browsers.

### Description

As a HEIC user, I want to navigate to the "Maintain Standard Part ICL" and "Standard Quality ID for Part" from mega menu irrespective of project, so that I can quickly access them.

### Acceptance Criteria

Functional: 
  1. While accessing "Maintain Standard part ICL" and "Standard Quality ID for Part" from Mega Menu, these features should open in a generic format without passing any project details. 
  2. User should be able to select projects or search project from dropdown. 
  3. There should not be any project selected by default. 
  4. Once project is selected then existing Part ICL shall be shown in table if any.

| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/14455) |

### Description

As a PLNG2,3, I want to access formulas in the Lifting Lug details page, so that I can view all the formulas involved for the calculation

### Acceptance Criteria

Functional: 
  1. User should be able to view "i" button as per visual: [Flow 21 (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/afbab5f0-f289-4442-ac86-2baf22d2251f-fae9/screen/abea34ca-8967-43bb-905c-cbe08941689e)
  2. Clicking on the "i" button should show the relevant formula as per visual 
  3. Clicking outside the formula will hide the formula

## US-15200 — CR-Delete Functionality

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-15200 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\QA_QC_NDE\Feature Team 1 |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2024-03-12T06:38:26.41Z |
| Created Date | 2023-09-21T08:26:28.863Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/15200) |

### Description

As a PLNG2/3, I want to access the delete functionality in the index screen of planning calculations, so that I can quickly delete any line item as per requirement

### Acceptance Criteria

Perquisite: 
User should have created a line item with status as Draft. 
  

Functional: 
  1. User should be able to see a delete icon on the line items with status as "Draft" on the index page of all the five calculations (Lifting Lug, Lifting Trunnion, Moon Plate, Ovality Ring, LSR/ISR). [Flow 21 (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/afbab5f0-f289-4442-ac86-2baf22d2251f-fae9/screen/1c8726f6-b1f2-4083-818f-38ed27f6ee34/)
  2. For all the line items with status as "Draft" on the index page, they should also see checkboxes next to them as per visual 
  3. At the top of the gird, user should be able to see a button for bulk delete as per visual 
  4. Methods to delete: 
     1. User should be able to delete a line item, by clicking on the in-line delete button as per visual. Clicking on it should show a confirmation popup, and on confirming the same a success toast message should be displayed, post which the line item should get deleted and the grid S.No should automatically aligned 
     2. Additionally, user can check the checkboxes and click on the bulk delete button. This will show a  confirmation popup, and on confirming the same a success toast message should be displayed, post which the line items selected should get deleted and the grid S.No should automatically aligned

## US-15201 — CR- LSR/ISR- Details Page

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-15201 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\QA_QC_NDE\Feature Team 1 |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2024-03-12T06:38:26.41Z |
| Created Date | 2023-09-21T08:31:14.98Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/15201) |

### Description

As a PLNG2/3, I want to keep the data intact even on the switching of Angle, so that I can quickly get relevant results

### Acceptance Criteria

Functional: 
  1. When the user is performing the calculations for LSR/ISR, then after entering inputs for a specific Angle (25x25 or 50x50), then if a user enter data under Angel 25x25 and gets output and now user switches to angel 50x50, then the systems should keep the inputs data intact and accordingly display the output as per angle 50x50 
  2. This is also possible for vice versa case 
  3. Please note that if a user has entered input and got output as per selected angle for OD Angle and then the user is switching to ID Angle, then the data will get lost and user will be required to enter all the inputs again

## US-15206 — CR- Duplicate Data Validation

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-15206 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\QA_QC_NDE\Feature Team 1 |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2024-03-12T06:38:26.41Z |
| Created Date | 2023-09-21T08:42:12.117Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/15206) |

### Description

As a PLNG2/3, I want to get notified while saving a data with same inputs wrt existing data, so that I can avoid duplicate data entries in the system

### Acceptance Criteria

Functional: 
  

For (Lifting Lug, Lifting Trunnion, Ovality Ring, LSR/ISR) planning calculations, when user is trying to save the data, then system should compare all the entered inputs with the inputs from the line items present in the system with status as "Completed". If the user inputs match completely with an already existing completed entry, then system should show an error message "Data already exist, please enter different input combination", and should not store the data in the system, unless user provides a unique input combination which is not present in the system.

### Acceptance Criteria

Module | Sub Process | Function | Locations | Roles   
---|---|---|---|---

| MCC-1/2/3, PMG-1/2/3, PUR-1/2/3

  2. Navigation: 
Mega Menu->Quality Assurance->Vendor Assessment-> Maintain Assessment Request 
  

  3. For Quick links, the name for this feature will be "Maintain Assessment Request"

| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/16360) |

### Description

As a MCC-1/2/3, PMG-1/2/3,PUR-1/2/3, user, I want to add a new assessment request in the system, so that I can maintain the data and send it for approval

### Acceptance Criteria

Functional: 
  1. User should be able to Add a new assessment request by clicking on "Add New" [Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/9b032036-82c4-469d-82b9-4d3c33cd61d3)  
Reference Image:  
  

  2. Clicking on the Add New button should open a right side over lay as per visual: [Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/9b032036-82c4-469d-82b9-4d3c33cd61d3)  
Reference Image:  
  

  3. User should be able to fill the data as below 
     1. **ASL Number: User input, maximum 50 characters, alphanumeric, optional value**
     2. **Supplier Code:  ****User input, maximum 50 characters, alphanumeric, optional value.  **
        1. Please note that above the Supplier Code label, there is a "i" symbol, hovering on it should display text as visible in the visual  
Reference Image:  
 
     3. **Supplier Name:  ****  User input, maximum 50 characters, alphanumeric, optional value. **
     4. Initiator: By default, the username will come who is creating the request. It will be in read only mode
     5. Initiator Date: By default, the date of creation of request should be populated here, it will be in read only mode (Format: dd/mm/yyyy)
     6. Notes (Optional): User input, maximum 100 characters, alphanumeric, optional value. 
  4. Once the user fills the data, then the user can Save the data by clicking on the Save button 
  5. Clicking on Save will should a success toast message as per visual and a new line item will get generated on the index grid 
  6. Also, as soon as Save is clicked, the user should be automatically taken to the details page 
  7. The Status of the line item will be "Draft"
  8. All the data as per user input will be populated in the index grid as per mentioned column names 
  9. In the "Draft" status, below action button will be visible 
     1. View 
     2. Timeline: It will have created line item along with name of the user and date/time stamp 
     3. Delete 
  10. The "Scope Recommended" will be greyed out as per visual in the Draft mode 
  11. The "Audit Result" will be blank in draft mode. This field will have value, only when the "Assessment Req status" for that line item becomes "Assessment Completed"
  12. In the Add new screen, clicking on Cancel of X button will close the overlay, without saving any data 
  13. If user has not entered any data for any column data, then that column entry will be blank and will come as hyphen

## US-16361 — Maintain Assessment Request \| Detail Screen 

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-16361 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2024-10-18T07:10:49.83Z |
| Created Date | 2023-10-16T06:22:18.313Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/16361) |

### Description

As a MCC-1/2/3, PMG-1/2/3, PUR-1/2/3 user, I want to maintain data for a generated line item, so that I can send it for approval

### Acceptance Criteria

Perquisite: 
User should have created a Maintain Assessment Request and generated a line item. The status should be either "Draft" or "Returned"
Functional:
  1. User can go to the details page of a line item by clicking on the View icon. On hovering this icon, the text should be displayed as per visual: [Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/9b032036-82c4-469d-82b9-4d3c33cd61d3)  
Reference Image: Please note that on hover of this image, a text needs to be shown as per visual  
  

  2. The detail screen in the "Draft" mode status, should be shown as per visual 
  3. The Heading, breadcrumbs and basic details (In read only mode) should be visible as per visual: [Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/ace8f752-5cd1-4d4f-a849-e9f870e52ce2)  
Reference Image:  
  

  4. Below the basic details, user should be able to see list of a grid with columns as below:  

     1. Sr. No. 
     2. Name of Document 
     3. Applicable 
  5. In this gird, under the "Name of Document" column, below 15 documents names should be always visible 
     1. Organization Chart  

     2. Copy of Certificates & accreditations  

     3. Quality System Manual & Procedures  

     4. Last Internal Audit Schedule & List of Internal Auditors  

     5. Procedure for Selection of Supplier, Approved Supplier List  

     6. List of Instruments for Inspection with Calibration Status (2 Sample Calibration report of M&TE)  

     7. Training Plan & Training Record  

     8. List of Qualified NDE Personnel  

     9. Procedure for Document Control, Material Traceability, Storage & Preservation of Raw Material/Finished Components  

     10. Process Flow from Receipt of Material to Dispatch of Finished item  

     11. Material Testing Facility  

     12. Procedure for Non-conformance & sample report (Filled)  

     13. Information on current defect occurrences & analysis  

     14. Sample Inspection Reports & Material Test Certificates  

     15. Procedure for Handling Customer complaint & evidence of satisfactory closure of Customer Complaint 
  6. The Sr. No against the Document name should progressively increase 
  7. Under the column named "Applicable", there will be a toggle switch, on every line item, which will be by-default in "On state"
  8. At the last, there will be a upload icon against all the line items as per visual. 
  9. At the top of the grid, there will be a "Add New" icon, clicking on it should generate a new line item in an editable mode as per visual, it should automatically generate the Sr.No as per current grid numbering sequence  
Reference Image:  
  

     1. The user can enter the name and check the toggle switch if required (By default the toggle switch will be in on state) and once the user is satisfied, the user can click on the tick icon, which will save the data in system 
     2. Clicking on the X button will not save any data, and will remove the created line item 
  10. Please note that the text present below grid "Total XX Documents", here the XX will get replaced by the total count of the line items, By default the count will be 15, but it may increase if the user add new line items. Please note that the count is the total count of the documents irrespective of the toggle switch status or uploaded documents 
  11. User should be able to user the search functionality, by clicking on the filter icon, clicking on it will open the search bar  
Reference Image:  
  

  12. User should be able to export the data by clicking on the export button 
     1. The data will be exported to excel with name as "Maintain Assessment Documents" and in the "Applicable" column, the values will be Yes/No. "Yes" will come if the toggle button is switched-on and "No" will come when the toggle button is switched-off 
  13. User should be able to use the column sorting functionality

### Description

As a MCC-1/2/3, PMG-1/2/3, PUR-1/2/3, user, I want to upload relevant documents in the Maintain Assessment Request, so that I can get it reviewed from the approver

### Acceptance Criteria

Perquisite: 
User should have created a Maintain Assessment Request and generated a line item. The status should be either "Draft" or "Returned"
  

Functional: 
  1. In the detail screen of a line item with status as "Draft" or "Returned", user should be able to upload documents as below: [Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/d8d06312-78ea-4bd0-ad8c-690892dd36fb)
     1. The user can click on the pin icon against the line item which will open the MVC controller for file upload/view/save/delete, where the user can upload and save the files 
     2. The uploaded files should get saved for that line item 
     3. Reference Image:  
  
  
  
  

     4. The user can switch off the toggle button as per requirement. 
  2. Please note that the data needs to be saved dynamically for the toggle switch on/off ,and the associated attached file, since there is no intermediary Save button

## US-16363 — Maintain Assessment Request \| Submit

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-16363 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2024-10-18T07:10:49.83Z |
| Created Date | 2023-10-16T06:46:40.037Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/16363) |

### Description

As a MCC-1/2/3, PMG-1/2/3, PUR-1/2/3 user, I want to submit the created request to the approver, so that it can get approved in the system

### Acceptance Criteria

Perquisite: 
User should have created a Maintain Assessment Request and generated a line item. The status should be either "Draft" or "Returned"
Functional: 
  1. User should be able to Submit the Maintain assessment Request from the details screen by clicking on the "Submit" button: [Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/d8d06312-78ea-4bd0-ad8c-690892dd36fb)
  2. The Submit button will be activated, when at least one documents toggle button is in switch on state and the corresponding pin color is green 
  3. After clicking on the submit button, there will be a confirmation popup and confirming the same will send all the line items along with the toggle states and the documents as it is, to the QA tray for the same location for approval. Also a success toast message will also be visible  
Reference Image:  
  

  4. In the Maintain Assessment Request index screen, the status will change to "Sent for Review": [Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/9b032036-82c4-469d-82b9-4d3c33cd61d3)  
  

  5. When the status is "Sent for Review", the line items timeline will be updated and below action button will be visible to the user 
     1. View 
     2. Timeline 
  6. Please note that the after clicking on the Submit button, then the assessment request should be submitted to the QA-2 of the same location as that of the initiator of the request. Also, all the line items with status of the toggle button (Yes/No) and the files uploaded needs to be sent. It may be possible that user has uploaded the file, but switched off the toggle button, still the files will not get deleted from the system

## US-16364 — Maintain Assessment Request \| Index Screen Grid View Components

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-16364 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2024-10-18T07:10:49.83Z |
| Created Date | 2023-10-16T06:47:27.48Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/16364) |

### Description

As a MCC-1/2/3, PMG-1/2/3, PUR-1/2/3 user, I want to access the additional functionalities in the maintain Assessment Request index screen, so that I can quickly perform the tasks

### Acceptance Criteria

Functional: 
  1. User should be able to access pagination functionality in the index screen: [Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/9b032036-82c4-469d-82b9-4d3c33cd61d3)
  2. User should be able to use the export functionality by clicking on the export button   
Reference Image:  
  

  3. User should be able to user the column sorting functionality as per visual: [Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/9b032036-82c4-469d-82b9-4d3c33cd61d3)
  4. User should be able to use the search functionality as per visual 
  5. User should be able to user the below filters 
     1. Status: Multiselect dropdown 
     2. Approver: Searchable Multiselect dropdown
     3. Supplier Name: Searchable Multiselect dropdown
  6. View Button: This will be visible all the time once a line item is generated 
  7. Timeline: This will be visible all the time once a line item is generated, clicking on it will open an over lay with timeline. Below will be the possible status: Please note that which each statuses, the name of the person and the date/time stamp should also be captured and displayed
     1. Draft:
     2. Sent for Review
     3. Accepted (Assessment Not Required)
     4. Accepted (Assessment Required)  

     5. Assessment Completed 
     6. Returned
     7. Cancelled
  8. Download (Pin icon) : This button will only be visible when the value for a line item under "Assessment Req status" column to be as
     1. Assessment Completed
  9. Cancel: This button will be accessible only in Returned state. Clicking on it will open a popup which will ask the user to enter remarks and after entering remarks and clicking Ok, will change the status of the line item to "Cancelled" state. In the case a line item has cancelled state, below action items will be visible, also, against the status, the returned remarks will be visible as per visual
     1. View
     2. Timeline  
Reference Image:  
  

  10. Delete: Delete button will be visible in Draft and Returned state. Clicking on it will show a confirmation popup and confirming the same will delete the line item 
  11. Audit Result Remarks: If remarks are available then it should be highlighted as per visual, clicking on it should open the tray to view the text 
  12. Scope Recommended: If remarks are available then it should be highlighted as per visual, clicking on it should open the tray to view the text
  13. Returned Remarks, If returned remarks are available, then it should be highlighted as per visual under the "Assessment Req. Status" column
  14. Pin Icon: This icon will only be visible with status as "Assessment Completed". Clicking on it should open the legacy portal upload/view controller and will show the uploaded files by QA in the section name "Upload Assessment Report". Please note that user will not be allowed to delete any files

### Description

As a MCC-1/2/3, PMG-1/2/3, PUR-1/2/3 user, I want to view the already existing line items from Maintain Assessment Request screen, so that I refer it for reference

### Acceptance Criteria

Functional: 
  1. The read only mode will be visible to user as per visual: [Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/f47f3c64-3863-4e29-839a-a44dc7e4789e) (Refer Page#3 of xd) 
  2. User should be able to view an already existing line items in read only mode for below status:

     2. Accepted-Assessment Required

     4. Assessment Completed 
     5. Re-Assessment required 
     6. Cancelled

     1. Add New 
     2. Submit: 
     3. Toggle Button 
     4. Pin 
  4. User will be allowed to access below functionalities 
     1. Export 
     2. Search 
     3. Column sorting 
  5. In the Applicable column, there will be two statuses viz "Yes" and "No", The toggle button on state will represent Yes and the toggle button off state will represent No 
  6. User will be able to see a download icon, clicking on this will download the file

## US-16435 — Assessment Request Review \| Index Screen-Pending Section

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-16435 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2024-10-18T07:10:49.83Z |
| Created Date | 2023-10-17T12:04:41.523Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/16435) |

### Description

As a QA-2, I want to view the "Assessment Request Review " pending tab screens index page as per my tagged location, so that I can quickly act on it

### Acceptance Criteria

Functional: 
  1. The index screen's breadcrumbs and heading should be visible as per visual: [Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/be2edef2-3fad-4878-901a-a9729b89edbd) The back button click will take the user to the immediate previous screen  
Reference Image:  
  

  2. There will be a switch bar with status as "Pending" and "All", by default, "Pending" need to be selected. [Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/be2edef2-3fad-4878-901a-a9729b89edbd) (Refer Page#9 of xd) 
  3. The grid column will be as per visual:  
Reference Image:  
  

  4. Pending Tab 
     1. The requests sent from the "Maintain Assessment Request" module, which are sent for review should be shown in the Pending section. Please note that this will be location specific. If the Logged in QA is from Hazira location tagged in the system, then the user will be able to see the request sent from the initiator who is also from same location 
     2. In the "Buyer's Note" column, the Notes which were written by the initiator of the request needs to be shown as per visual  

     3. Rest of the details needs to be fetched and shown as per data from the "Maintain Assessment Request" module as per location
     4. In case there is no data for a specific column other then Buyer's note column, there will be a hyphen. In case of Buyer's note, if there is no data, then a greyed out image will be shown as per visual
     5. In the action button column, there will be only one button which is "View" button

| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/16443) |

### Description

As a QA-2, I want to quickly review the items sent to my tray as per location, so that I can review it

### Acceptance Criteria

Functional: 
  1. Under the Pending tab, all the line items will have view button, clicking on it will open the details page as per visual: [Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/2d9f5041-32c8-42ac-95f2-68f5e273cea5) (Refer Page#12 of xd) 
  2. User should be able to see the breadcrumbs, back button which will take the user to the immediate back screen:  
Reference Image:  
  

  3. User should be able to see the basic details in read only mode:  
Reference Image:  
  

  4. User should be able to view the grid as per visual: [Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/2d9f5041-32c8-42ac-95f2-68f5e273cea5)  

  5. In the grid, the all the documents will be visible as per the request was maintained and sent by the initiator. 
  6. **Against the line item, the one's which have the documents uploaded from the "Maintain Assessment Request" module will have a download button. Clicking on it will download the uploaded file. It may be possible that the status may be No, but the file is present, then also, the download button needs to be shown, which will download the uploaded document**
  7. If a user want to download all the documents, then user can click on the "Download All" button, which should download all the documents in a zip file format 
  8. User should be able to perform column sorting functionality 
  9. User should be able to perform search functionality 
  10. User will be able to the see the count of total documents below the grid 
  11. User will be able to see below buttons below the grid: [Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/2d9f5041-32c8-42ac-95f2-68f5e273cea5)
     1. Close 
     2. Return 
     3. Proceed with Assessment 
     4. Proceed without Assessment (Please note that this button will be hidden under arrow)

### Description

As a QA-2, I want to return a pending items came to my tray, so that the details can be captured in the system

### Acceptance Criteria

Perquisite: US#16443 
Functional: 
  1. The action button present under the grid in the details page will function as below 
     1. Close: This will take the user to the index page of "Assessment Request Review"
     2. Return: 
        1. This button will open a popup dialogue to mandatorily write remarks, and post a success toast message will be displayed. The status of that line item will be changed to "Returned" in the "Assessment Request Review" Index screen and the line item will be moved from "Pending" to "All" try. Also, once the line item is returned, then in the details screen, beside the Returned status, the returned remarks will start becoming visible ([Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/b3312a23-e362-46d1-b439-ab54550558ac)) (Refer Page#10 of xd) Clicking on the returned remarks, will open the remarks entered . Also, the action button at the bottom of the grid in the detail section "Assessment Details" will now only have one button, which will be "Close": [Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/2d9f5041-32c8-42ac-95f2-68f5e273cea5)  
Reference Image:  
  
  
  

        2. For this returned line item, in the "Maintain Assessment Request" index screen, the status will also change to "Returned" along with the returned remarks next to it, Clicking on the returned remarks, will open the remarks entered. Also, this will also generate a Returned status in the timeline: [Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/9b032036-82c4-469d-82b9-4d3c33cd61d3) (Refer Page#2)

  2. Confirming the same, will show a success toast message as per visual, also below things will happen
     1. In the index page of "Assessment Request Review", the status will change to "Accepted (Assessment Required)" for that line item: [Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/b3312a23-e362-46d1-b439-ab54550558ac)
     2. Also, in the details page of "Assessment Details", a new tab named "Assessment Schedule & Result" will get created as per visual: [Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/560e9820-69eb-4e27-8c9b-c31ea9e80995) (Refer page#15 of xd)  
Reference Image:  
  

     3. In the index page of "Maintain Assessment Request" the status will change to "Accepted (Assessment Required)" for that line item, and in the timeline, one more line item will get appended with status as "Accepted (Assessment Required)"" along with date/time stamp and the QA persons name: [Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/9b032036-82c4-469d-82b9-4d3c33cd61d3)

| Field | Value |

| Changed Date | 2024-10-18T07:10:49.83Z |
| Created Date | 2023-10-17T13:13:44.45Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/16447) |

### Description

As a QA-2, I want to schedule and maintain assessment related data for the vendor, so that it can get captured in the system

### Acceptance Criteria

Perquisite: US#16446  

Functional:
  1. Under the tab named "Assessment Schedule & Result", user should be able to see the first section named "Schedule of Assessment"
     1. In this section, there will two components: [Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/560e9820-69eb-4e27-8c9b-c31ea9e80995) (Refer Page#15 of xd)
        1. Date Picker: By default the current date should be shown, but the user can select future date 
        2. Time (Optional) By default the current time should be shown, but user can select custom time  
Reference Image:  
  

        3. Post, this, the "Email to Initiator" button will get activated. Clicking on it will show a success toast message as per visual and will trigger an email to the initiator, who has created this request.**(For Email format, please refer ADO attachment)**
        4. Please note that on click of Email to initiator, the system should also save the values  in system, since there is no intermediary save button
  2. ​The next section will be named "Auditor for Assessment"  
Reference Image:  
  

     1. Here there will be following components 
        1. Auditor: User can search and select relevant Auditor, multi select is possible (System to fetch and show all IEMQS users) 
        2. Pin Icon (View/Attach Documents): Clicking on this will open the legacy MVC, upload/download controller, where the user can upload/save/delete the documents  
Reference Image:  
  
  
  

        3. Once the user has selected the Auditor/Auditor's and attached the documents, then the "Email to Auditor" button will get enabled 
        4. Clicking on that button will do the following 
           1. Firstly, a confirmation popup should be shown with content "Are you sure you have attached all the relevant documents to be sent to the Auditor/s" along with "Yes" and "No" button, clicking on Yes will trigger an E-mail request and show a success toast message as per visual :[Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/560e9820-69eb-4e27-8c9b-c31ea9e80995)  
Reference Image:  
  

           2. Please note that on click of Email to Auditor, the system should also save the values in system, since there is no intermediary save button  

           3. Clicking on "No", will close the popup 
  3. Next section will be named "Upload Assessment Report"  
Reference Image:  
  

     1. Here there will be a pin button (View/Attach Document): Clicking on this will open the legacy MVC, upload/download controller, where the user can upload/save/delete the documents
  4. The last section will be named as "Assessment Result", which will have below components: 
     1. Recommended For: Single select dropdown, Values
        1. Acceptable 
        2. Conditionally Acceptable 
        3. Rejected 
     2. Scope Recommended: User Input, text area, maximum 200 characters, alphanumeric. 
  5. The "Complete" button will be enabled once the user has uploaded file in the "Upload Assessment Report" section and selected the "Recommended For" dropdown value and filled the "Scope Recommended"
  6. Clicking on "Complete" will show a confirmation popup, and confirming the same will show a success toast message and do the following things:   
Reference Image:  
  

     1. It will change the status of the line item from the index page of "Assessment Request Review" to "Assessment Completed", [Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/b3312a23-e362-46d1-b439-ab54550558ac). Also, in its details screen, the "Complete" button will now get hidden [Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/9221c55d-7f06-4eb0-a117-6e5e2a7c4148), (Refer Page#18 of xd) 
     2. Also, from the index page of "Maintain Assessment Request" module ([Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/9b032036-82c4-469d-82b9-4d3c33cd61d3)),  the status for that line item will change to "Assessment Completed". Also a line item in the timeline will get populated with the status, name and date/timestamp. Also, the "Approver" and "Approver Date" will be populated
     3. In the index page of "Maintain Assessment Request" module, for that line item in the action button, a pin icon will now be shown, clicking on it open the MVC controller for upload/view files, where the user will only be able to view and download the files. These files needs to be fetched from the "Upload Assessment Report" section ([Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/9221c55d-7f06-4eb0-a117-6e5e2a7c4148))
     4. The "Scope Recommended" which is filled in the "Assessment Details" sessiond, should be visible in the index page of "Maintain Assessment Request" module, under the "Scope Recommended" column  

     5. In the "Maintain Assessment Request" index page, under the "Audit Result" column, the value selected from the "Recommended For" under the "Assessment Result" section of "Assessment Details" screen should be visible
  7. Once the item is completed, the action button at the bottom of the grid in the detail section "Assessment Details" will now only have one button, which will be "Close" ([Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/9221c55d-7f06-4eb0-a117-6e5e2a7c4148))

### Description

### Acceptance Criteria

Perquisite: 
  1. User should have received a assessment request in the pending tray, 
  2. US#16443  



Functional: 
  1. User can click on the view item with status as "Sent for Review" from the pending tray: [Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/be2edef2-3fad-4878-901a-a9729b89edbd)
  2. Clicking on it will show the Assessment Details page as per visual: [Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/2d9f5041-32c8-42ac-95f2-68f5e273cea5)
  3. All the functionalities needs to be followed as per US#16443 
  4. Once the user has reviewed the documents and the user wants to complete the line item without taking any assessment, then the user can click on the button named "Proceed without Assessment" which is hidden under the arrow button   
Reference Image:  
  

  5. Clicking on it will show a confirmation popup, and confirming the same will also show a success toast message as per visual: [Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/2d9f5041-32c8-42ac-95f2-68f5e273cea5)  
Reference Image:  
  

  6. The status for that line item will change as below: 
     1. In the index page of "Assessment Request Review", the status will change to "Assessment Completed(Assessment Not Required)": [Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/b3312a23-e362-46d1-b439-ab54550558ac) (Refer Page#10 of xd) 
     2. In the index page of "Maintain Assessment Request" the status will change to "Assessment Completed(Assessment Not Required)". Also a line item in the timeline will get populated with the status , name and date/timestamp. Also, the "Approver" and "Approver Date" will be populated: [Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/9b032036-82c4-469d-82b9-4d3c33cd61d3) (Refer Page#2 of xd). Please note that, the approver and approver date will be the approver who approved the request and the date on which the line item for approved
  7. Once the item is completed, the action button at the bottom of the grid in the detail section "Assessment Details" will now only have one button, which will be "Close": [Vendor assessment (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/593f662e-642d-4ebd-9549-afeb4a23ce80-0c6c/screen/bc5009f7-a2ef-481b-907e-75504bc25b2c) (Refer Page#23)

## US-16455 — Production - Identification marking need to enter at each set-up stage.

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-16455 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | TRISHUL TANDEL |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2024-10-18T07:10:49.83Z |
| Created Date | 2023-10-18T06:53:00.087Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/16455) |

### Description

* **_Identification Marking_** data shall be copied from _SET-UP & DIMENSION REPORT FOR ROLLED SHELL LONGITUDINAL SEAM_ (**_Protocol036_**) to _SET-UP & DIMENSION REPORT FOR CIRCUMFERENTIAL SEAM_ (**_Protocol037_**)
  * This changes will be done in _SET-UP & DIMENSION REPORT FOR CIRCUMFERENTIAL SEAM_ (**_Protocol037_**) web page. 
  * Trigger point of fetch this data, while user come first time on _SET-UP & DIMENSION REPORT FOR CIRCUMFERENTIAL SEAM_ (**_Protocol037_**)

### Acceptance Criteria

* System will fetch data based on part/position associate with the seam.
    * E.g. for project S040911, if require data of Identification Marking for CW1002 then for _SET-UP & DIMENSION REPORT FOR CIRCUMFERENTIAL SEAM_ (**_Protocol037_**), system will fetch data from associate part/position (1001,1002)
    * System will fetch seams which is associate with position 1001,1002. Like 
      * LW1001 - 1001,1001
      * LW1003 - 1002,1002
    * Now system will fetch Identification Marking data from setup protocol for LW1001 and LW1003.
    * All identification marking will be added in _SET-UP & DIMENSION REPORT FOR CIRCUMFERENTIAL SEAM_ (**_Protocol037_**)

## US-16554 — UAT Feedback#3

| Field | Value |

| Changed Date | 2023-12-25T05:38:10.74Z |
| Created Date | 2023-10-20T05:11:10.227Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/16554) |

### Description

As a NDE user, I want to view the latest NDE screen, so that I can quicky view the dashboards details.  

Also, As a Prod/QC/NDE, I want to access the expand/collapse side bar as per latest prototype, so that I can quickly see the relevant data  
Also, as a Prod/QC/NDE, I want to view the shop code and shop description together in the Shop wise filter, so that I can get the correct details of the selected value

## US-17348 — Design - In DIS & SIN report only latest revision of document is shown.

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-17348 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | TRISHUL TANDEL |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2024-10-18T07:10:49.83Z |
| Created Date | 2023-11-23T13:19:33.42Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/17348) |

### Description

In DIS & SIN report last three revisions of documents in last three issue & "continued from-CF" shall be shown similar to DIN  

  

Logic: 
  * Edited by and Edited On field shall be updated while "Create SIN" from approved DIS 
  * Add new "DocumentStatus" column in SIN document list. Value shall be as per below: 
    * If any change in EditedOn column of SIN documents, then Document Status shall be "Revised"
    * if add new document. then Document Status shall be "Added"
    * if no change with previous SIN, then Document Status shall be "\-->"
  * Add new column in SIN report for display "Document Status". 


  

Two more points: 
1) system shall not allow any change if DIS status is "Approved". 
2) new documents shall be added last in the list of documents.

### Acceptance Criteria

_Not provided._

### Description

* Email shall be sent once approve WCBL.


 
**Email shall be sent to following recipients in “To”:**
  1. WMMC Email id: [rajeshkumar.behera@larsentoubro.com](mailto:rajeshkumar.behera@larsentoubro.com "mailto:rajeshkumar.behera@larsentoubro.com") (This will be fetched from CONFIG table)

  1. WE-3 for respective Project - who has submitted WCBL.
  2. WE-2 for respective Project – who has Approved WCBL

##Project No.##-Welding Consumable Batch List (WCBL)_##Rev. No.## _(Example, S041040A-Consumable Batch List  (WCBL)_R3)_

Dear All,
 
Consumable Batch List (WCBL)-##Project Number##_##Rev No.## is released by WE in IEMQS. _(Example,  Consumable Batch List (WCBL)-S041040A_R3)_
Please check.
 
_Click Here_  for more details.
 
 
Regards,
IEMQS System

### Description

* Changes will be done in below report as per mentioned:


 
Logic:    
  * Total Seams: count of total seams (Ref. QMS012)
  * Seam List
    * Approved: count of total approved seams (Ref. QMS012 – “Status”= “Approved”)
    * Pending: count of total pending seams (Ref. QMS012 – “Status” = “Draft”, “Returned”, “Sent For Approval”)
  * Test Plan
    * Approved: count of total approved seams (Ref. QMS020) – Status “Approved”
    * Pending: count of total pending seams (Ref. QMS020 – “Status” = “Draft”, “Returned”, “Sent For Approval”)


 
  * Shop Weld Plan
    * Approved: count of total approved seams (Ref. SWP010) – Status “Approved”
    * Pending: count of total pending seams (Ref. SWP010 – “Status” = “Draft”, “Returned”, “Sent For Approval”)


 
  * ICL List
    * Approved: count of total approved seams (Ref. QMS030) – Status “Approved”
    * Pending: count of total pending seams (Ref. QMS030 – “Status” = “Draft”, “Returned”, “Sent For Approval”)

### Description

As **IEMQS**** ** User, I want to get project wise Cost of Poor Quality in the report, so that I can analyze the repair cost

### Acceptance Criteria

Refer attached FD file and COPQ Monitoring session excel file.  

  

~~Navigation: Refer attached excel.~~
~~  
~~
~~Functional:~~
  1. ~~User should be able to generate the report as per below mentioned logic:~~
  2. ~~Create a New DB Master Table as per below structure:~~
     1. ~~Production Centre~~ | ~~Stage Code~~ | ~~ReNDTCost~~ | ~~ManHrsCost ~~ | ~~OpportunityCost~~  
---|---|---|---|---  
~~  
~~
     2. ~~Please refer attached excel (Refer "Master Table" sheet) for dummy records ~~
  3. ~~Steps to calculate COPQ:  
~~
     1. ~~Fetch no. of repair spots details per NDT type (A-scan UT, RT & PAUT/TOFD)  
~~
        1. ~~Refer DB: QMS070, refer column name SPOT RESULT= REJECTED, only applicable for iteration 1 (refer field name=iteration number)~~
        2. ~~Scenario: In case of a seam ICL having both RT & A scan UT stage, following methodology to be used for COPQ:  
~~
           1. ~~Opportunity cost logic: This will be calculated based on the NDT stage which has max repair spots (Ex: UT repair spot = 5 & RT repair spots = 9, then no. of repair spots in RT will be used for opportunity cost calculation)   ~~
           2. ~~Consumable + re-NDT cost logic:    ~~
              1. ~~Ratio of no of repair spots in UT to no. of repair spots in RT (UT/RT) > = 5 | Then use the UT cost of consumables & Re-NDT  ~~
              2. ~~Ratio of no of repair spots in UT to no. of repair spots in RT (UT/RT) < = 5 | Then use the RT cost of consumables & Re-NDT  ~~
              3. ~~Sum of   "Actual Consumable + re-NDT" for repair spots in RT and  "Actual Consumable + re-NDT" for repair spots in UT.  
~~
     2. ~~Compare the RT and UT stage from Stage Master, it should be before PWHT (Pre Heat Treatment), refer below screenshot s.no2  
  
~~
     3. ~~[Cost of consumables + Re-NDT] = [No. of repair spots * Consumables & Re-NDT cost]  
~~
     4. ~~[Opportunity cost due to manhour loss in repair] = [No. of repair spots * Man-hours taken per repair * Opportunity cost as per Production Center]  
~~
     5. ~~COPQ = [Cost of consumables & Re-NDT] + [Opportunity cost due to manhour loss in repair]  
~~
     6. ~~Display Note at the bottom of the screen & on report: No. of seams [count of seams (seam ICL containing RT & UT stages and either of them is balance)] has not been considered in COPQ calculation since RT or UT result is balance (This is static note). This note is applicable when seam contain both RT & UT stage in parallel. Upon clicking "Count of seam" system shall show project no, seam number, stage. ~~
     7. ~~System shall show, list of seams for which COPQ has not been calculated due to unavailability of master data~~
     8. ~~Upon clicking on seams, system should provide list of seams that don't have COPQ data available~~
  4. ~~How to identify ASCAN UT, PAUT and TOFDUT?~~
     1. ~~Use Table QMS060~~
     2. ~~Pass the request ID from QMS070 table, to get the stage type~~
  5. ~~Refer the attached excel, for calculation and logic~~

## US-17690 — #2 COPQ \| Master \| Maintain Weld Repair NDT Cost

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-17690 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0\Sprint 14 |
| Changed Date | 2024-10-18T07:10:49.83Z |
| Created Date | 2023-12-14T04:06:29.993Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/17690) |

### Description

As **QA-1/2/3**** ** User, I want to access the master page for maintaining parameters for calculating Cost of Poor Quality, so that I can get the report accordingly

### Acceptance Criteria

**Perquisite:** US#17682 
  

**Functional:**
**
  1. If a user wants to add a new record, then the user can do so by clicking on the "Add New" button 
  2. Clicking on the "Add New" button should add a new line item in the grid at the top as per visual: [Flow 2 (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/67c87a39-350a-4c79-9af5-d2f778cc401f-329b/screen/e6b39b21-1bc7-403e-9c19-c5edfe9d0643)
  3. The validations will be as below 
     1. Production Center: Search and select. single selection allowed 
        1. Refer DB for fetching values:
     2. Stage Code: Search and select. single selection allowed 
        1. Refer DB for fetching values:
     3. Repair Cost (INR): User Input. No decimal allowed. Value range- from y or x till xxxxx digits (Please note that the value of y can be 0 and value of x can range from 0-9) 
     4. No. of Man Hours: User Input. No decimal allowed. Value range- from y or x till xx digits (Please note that the value of y can be 0 and value of x can range from 0-9)
     5. Opportunity Cost: User Input. No decimal allowed. Value range- from y or x till xxxxx digits (Please note that the value of y can be 0 and value of x can range from 0-9)
  4. Once the user enters all the above-mentioned values, then the Save button (green tick) should get activated. 
     1. Clicking on save will show a success toast message as per visual and a new line item will get populated in the grid as well as DB 
     2. Once the line items is saved, then the "Updated By" and "Updated On" should be updated as per logged in user details and the current date  

     3. Also, once a line item is saved, then beside that line item, two action buttons should be visible. 
        1. Edit: Clicking on it will make the selected line item editable, in which the user can modify the details and again click on the save button. Please note that in this case, the "Updated By" and "Updated On" will also get updated accordingly 
        2. Delete: Clicking in this will open a confirmation popup as per visual ([Flow 2 (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/67c87a39-350a-4c79-9af5-d2f778cc401f-329b/screen/65f38a78-8d70-4901-b10c-43cfce9ec849)), and confirming the same will show a success toast message and delete the selected line item. (Please note that it will be a hard delete and from DB as well the data should ger removed) 
  5. If a user wants to delete the line before saving it, then the user can click on the red X button 
  6. The gird should have pagination functionality as per visual 

  
**
