## US-17714 — Production - Dashboard to be made in I-Rudra that shows Total seams/Before-After PWHT/Hydro/ Pending or approved seam count.

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-17714 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | TRISHUL TANDEL |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2024-10-18T07:10:49.83Z |
| Created Date | 2023-12-18T16:58:07.92Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/17714) |

### Description

Telerik Chart URL:  <https://demos.telerik.com/aspnet-core/bar-charts/stacked-bar> 
                
  * <https://vhzdigiiemqsqa1.lthed.com:447/IPI/MaintainSeamListOfferInspection/Index> 


  

  

  

 
  * Require Section/Assembly selection option, so user can filter data. Default all option will select and data will populate based on selection. 
  * Display count on bar. 


 
  1. By default, two chart shall be displayed as per selection of Project and Assembly. 
     1. Milestone Chart 
     2. Inspection Status Chart 
  2. Clicking upon any horizontal bar of Milestone Chart, “Inspection Status Chart” shall be refreshed and displayed based on selection of Project, Assembly and Milestone.  
  3. Clicking upon any horizontal bar of Inspection Status Chart, system will open respective tab as per selection area(Color) 
     1. If clicking upon Red (Rejected) color bar, system will open respective tab based on stage type 
        1. If stage type belongs to QC, then “Re-Offer” tab will appear with selected filters. 
        2. If stage type belongs to NDE, then “Regenerate Request” tab will appear with selected filters. 
     2. If clicking upon Pink (Returned) color bar, system will open respective tab based on stage type 
        1. If stage type belongs to QC, then “Re-Offer” tab will appear with selected filters. 
        2. If stage type belongs to NDE, then “Return NDE Request” tab will appear with selected filters. 
     3. If clicking upon Yellow (Ready to Offer) color bar, then “Offer” tab will appear with selected filters. 
     4. If clicking upon Green (Cleared), Orange (Under Inspection) and Balance (Gray) color bar, then “All” tab will appear with selected filters. 


 
 
                
 
**Chart1:  **
<div class="demo-section wide"> 
    @(Html.Kendo().Chart() 
        .Name("chart") 
        .Title("") 
        .Legend(legend => legend 
            .Position(ChartLegendPosition.Right) 
        ) 
        .SeriesDefaults(seriesDefaults => 
            seriesDefaults.Bar().Stack(true) 
        ) 
        .Series(series => { 
            series.Bar(new double[] { 900, 200, 0 }).Name("Cleared").Color("#AED581"); 
            series.Bar(new double[] { 0, 200, 300 }).Name("Inprogress").Color("#FFCC80"); 
            series.Bar(new double[] { 0, 100, 500 }).Name("Balance").Color("#CFD8DC"); 
        }) 
        .CategoryAxis(axis => axis 
            .Categories("Before PWHT", "After PWHT", "After Hydro") 
            .MajorGridLines(lines => lines.Visible(false)) 
        ) 
        .ValueAxis(axis => axis 
            .Numeric() 
            .Labels(labels => labels.Format("{0}")) 
            .Max(500) 
            .Line(line => line.Visible(false)) 
            .MajorGridLines(lines => lines.Visible(false)) 
        ) 
        .Tooltip(tooltip => tooltip 
            .Visible(true) 
            .Template("#= series.name #: #= value #") 
        ) 
    )  
</div> 
 
 
Chart2: 
<div class="demo-section wide"> 
    @(Html.Kendo().Chart() 
        .Name("chart") 
        .Title("") 
        .Legend(legend => legend 
            .Position(ChartLegendPosition.Bottom) 
        ) 
        .SeriesDefaults(seriesDefaults => 
            seriesDefaults.Bar().Stack(true) 
        ) 
        .Series(series => { 
            series.Bar(new double[] { 500, 450, 0, 200, 500, 450, 0, 200, 0, 200  }).Name("Cleared").Color("#AED581"); 
            series.Bar(new double[] { 300, 50, 300, 400, 300, 50, 300, 400, 100, 20 }).Name("Under Inspection").Color("#FFCC80"); 
            series.Bar(new double[] { 0, 0, 10, 25, 25, 60, 0, 0, 70, 50 }).Name("Returned").Color("#F8BBD0"); 
            series.Bar(new double[] { 0, 0, 0, 15, 0, 60, 100, 50, 70, 50}).Name("Rejected").Color("#E57373"); 
            series.Bar(new double[] { 200, 500, 500, 100, 200, 500, 500, 100, 50, 70 }).Name("Ready to Offer").Color("#ffff9d"); 
            series.Bar(new double[] { 200, 500, 500, 100, 200, 500, 500, 100, 50, 75 }).Name("Balance").Color("#CFD8DC"); 
        }) 
        .CategoryAxis(axis => axis 
            .Categories("Setup", "Weld Visual", "Ferrite", "Checmical", "PMI", "Hardness", "PT", "MT", "RT", "UT") 
            .MajorGridLines(lines => lines.Visible(false)) 
        ) 
        .ValueAxis(axis => axis 
            .Numeric() 
            .Labels(labels => labels.Format("{0}")) 
            .Max(1000) 
            .Line(line => line.Visible(false)) 
            .MajorGridLines(lines => lines.Visible(false)) 
        ) 
        .Tooltip(tooltip => tooltip 
            .Visible(true) 
            .Template("#= series.name #: #= value #") 
        ) 
    )  
</div>

### Acceptance Criteria

_Not provided._

## US-17752 — SWRP UAT issues

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-17752 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\QA_QC_NDE\Feature Team 3 |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0\Sprint 18 |
| Changed Date | 2023-12-29T05:29:31.14Z |
| Created Date | 2023-12-21T11:05:23.403Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/17752) |

### Description

PFA points. 
All points are explained to POD & Quality teams.

### Acceptance Criteria

Point 1: 
  * User should be able to change ASME stamp and R Stamp after clicking on generate button (In “SWRP Details” after clicking “Generate” button only ASME Stamp, ‘R’ Stamp shall be editable) 
  * The generate button should be disabled after generating number. 
  * User should not be able to change categories and customer after generating number. 


Point 2: 
  * User should be able to input "Preheat temperature" manually. It should not be fetched from WPS automatically. 


Point 3: 
  * Label change : from - “Pre-Heat Temp°C min”  to “Pre-Heat Temp°C Min” 


Point 4: 
  * User should be able to get all welders' stamp in vertical list. 
  * System should match project no. and seam no. to get welder's list. 
  * User should be able to select welders' stamp multiple selection 


Point 5: 
  * Reduce width of columns, fields names : Size (mm), AMPS (A), Volt (V) 


Point 6: 
  * Project description (job description) should be fetched automatically. 


Point 7: 
  * Printout issues are marked in pdf file. Please refer attached pdf file. 


Point 8: 
  * On index page, while reviewing last record, system doesn't show more (three dots) option. Ux issue.

## US-17906 — Maintain RCA \| Redirection

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-17906 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\QA_QC_NDE\Feature Team 2 |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0\Sprint 16 |
| Changed Date | 2024-04-19T07:17:30.84Z |
| Created Date | 2024-01-03T03:55:09.047Z |
| Tags | CR |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/17906) |

### Description

RCA redirection in case of NCR/CFAR

### Acceptance Criteria

Functional: 
  

For creation of RCA in case of category is NCR and CFAR, below logic needs to be implemented: 
  1. Once the user comes to the MVC page of "CAPA submission" and clicks on "Maintain RCA" button, 
     1. User creates RCA in angular screen and save the record. When the user again visits the MVC page, then he should be able to see the RCA page linked with NCR/CFAR. 
     2. If the user again the clicks on the "Maintain RCA" button, and the RCA is "In-progress" status in Angular screen, then the user should be redirected to the same RCA page (Duplicate entry should not happen) 
  2. When user clicks on "Maintain RCA" on MVC page system shall open RCA angular page in new tab.

## US-17991 — CR: E-sign \| Investigate Team and Methodology

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-17991 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\QA_QC_NDE\Feature Team 2 |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0\Sprint 16 |
| Changed Date | 2024-03-18T10:56:14.917Z |
| Created Date | 2024-01-09T10:55:21.553Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/17991) |

### Description

As a user, I want to get e-sign/manual sign functionality in Investigate Team and Methodology, so that I can quickly get it signed

### Acceptance Criteria

Functional: 
  1. In the Maintain RCA session, under the Investigate Team and Methodology section, below needs to be done 
     1. The "Investigate Team" dropdown needs to changes to single select search and select dropdown 
     2. Beside "Investigate Team", a new field named "Functional Title" needs to be added which will be a text box (Maximum 100 characters, alphabetical) 
     3. Above the "Investigate Team" field, a button named "\+ Add Team Member" needs to be added 
        1. Clicking on this should generate a new row item with fields: Investigate Team and Functional Title 
        2. The second row onwards, a delete icon will be shown against the line items, clicking on it will delete the row 
     4. The Methodology Used field will come below the Investigate Team. It will dynamically shift down as per new rows are added 
  2. Just below the Main heading "Investigate Team and Methodology", a toggle button named "Digital Signature" should be place  
Reference Image:  
  

     1. This toggle button will be in On state by default 
     2. When this toggle button is in On state, then in the rows, signature space should be shown as below:  
  

     3. Clicking on the signature area should open the Wacom device capability to e-sign, and once the user sign, the name, as well as date time stamp should get captured on session as well as on the document 
     4. If the user switches Off the toggle button, then the signature pane should be hidden from the rows

## US-18037 — Welding - WML - Replace PS Number

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-18037 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | TRISHUL TANDEL |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2024-10-18T07:10:53.79Z |
| Created Date | 2024-01-16T06:55:21.76Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/18037) |

### Description

**__**
**_Develop system to transfer welder qualification of Advance / Temporary Welders, when they are added by IR as permanent  _****_employees._**
  * This feature will be implemented in WML list page. 


  * New window popup screen will be there as per below:
  * From PS No. - User will select (Dropdown list)
  * To PS No. - User will select (Dropdown list)
  * WML Type - User will select (Dropdown list)
  * Project \- User will select (Dropdown list)
    * This will be applicable if user select WML Type "Project Specific."
  * Replace (button) - 
    * System will ask confirmation message before proceeding the data.
      * "Are you sure to replace this welder with new PS no?"
    * If "Yes" \- then 
      * system will create new welder with new PS no and copy all WML data from old welder PS no.
      * system will mark Evalution flag 0/false for old PS no welder.
      * system will mark Evalution flag 1/true for new PS no welder.
      * system will close the Replace PS No window popup and refresh the WML list.
    * if "No" \- then
      * system will not perform any action, just close the confirmation popup.
  * Close (button)- 
    * System will close the Replace PS No. popup window.


**_  
_**
**_  
_**

### Acceptance Criteria

_Not provided._

## US-18099 — CR: Auto Fetch of Date in Certificate

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-18099 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\Common-Feature Team 1 |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0\Sprint 16 |
| Changed Date | 2024-03-12T06:38:31.183Z |
| Created Date | 2024-01-24T06:33:29.68Z |
| Tags | CR |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/18099) |

### Description

Auto fetch logic 
Cosmetic Changes

### Acceptance Criteria

Functional 
  1. While generating a certificate for user, provided the user has already generated the certificate one time (for more than R0), then in the certificate generation session, below needs to be implemented 
     1. Under "Employee Experience" section, in the "Experience Before L&T" field, system should automatically increase the experience as per certificate validity. For eg., if for R0 certificate generation, user has entered experience as 1 year and selected the certification validity as 3 years, then when the user again comes for recertification after three years, then systems should calculate and increase the "Experience Before L&T" as 4 Years. This will keep on increasing whenever user comes for recertification 
  2. For the field named "Experience in L&T" for recertification case (more then R0), systems should calculate and increase the years/months as per certificate validity. For eg, if a user has selected "Experience in L&T" as year while generating R0 certificate, and the certificate validity selected by the user is 3 years, then after 3 years when the user comes for recertification, then the value for "Experience in L&T" should become 4 years 


  

Cosmetic Changes:  

  1. In the training certification upload session, when the user is uploading the internal certification, then the popup heading should be changed from "Upload External Certification" to "Upload Training Certificate". Reference Link: [NDE Qualification Certification (adobe.com)](https://xd.adobe.com/view/02032730-76de-411b-98bb-cac84a33f59e-5750/screen/0dbf6372-855e-451c-baad-15e607ffe2bb)  
  

  2. In the calendar picker for upload the training certification, there should be validation that future date should not be allowed to be selected by the user

  *   



  * Clicking upon “Milestone” button, system will open popup like below:


         
  

  * New Milestone table needs to be created with below fields in ProtonDB database. Table name shall be “ProjectMilestone”.
    * **UIDMilestonePammingId** – int (Primary Key, Auto increment)
    * **Project** – nvarchar(20) – not null
    * **UID** – int (not null)
    * **Milestone** – nvarchar(1000) – not null
    * **Percentage** – float – not null
    * **CompletionDate** – datetime – nullable
    * **CreatedOn** – datetime (current datetime)
    * **CreatedBy** – userid of logged in user need to capture.  
  
 
  * Bind the data from above table in listing of above popup.

| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | TRISHUL TANDEL |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2024-10-18T07:10:53.79Z |
| Created Date | 2024-03-04T15:30:22.283Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/18366) |

### Description

\- New field needs to be introduced “Shop Release” (Yes/No) option. By default, document type “L&T Manufacturing Drawing”, “L&T Procedure”, “Shipping Document”, “GA Drawing” shall have “Yes” option selected while adding in JEP.
  

\- New field “JEP Number” selection option needs to be added in DIN web page. This fields shall be non-mandatory and editable always. 
  

\- Two new filters required on JEP web page:
o Document Type
o Latest Revision
  

\- Export to Excel option require for export all/filtered data. Column shall be same as display in JEP list
  

\- Shop release column shall have select all option to bulk update.
  

\- Tick shall be carried for next revisions.

### Acceptance Criteria

teset

## US-18505 — Project Specific Redirection \| Quicklinks

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-18505 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\QA_QC_NDE\Feature Team 2 |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0\Sprint 13 |
| Changed Date | 2024-03-20T12:22:52.453Z |
| Created Date | 2024-03-20T12:15:08.21Z |
| Tags | CR |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/18505) |

### Description

As a user, I want to access the Digital Vertical checklist session as per project number while accessing from Quicklinks, so that I can quickly access the relevant information

### Acceptance Criteria

Functional: 
  1. When the user tries to access Digital Vertical Checklist from Quicklinks, then system shuold open the session and show line items specific to the selected favorite project  
Eg:   
  
Here, the selected favorite project is S041020, so system should append it in the search bar filter, and should show relevant line items associated to that project, also, the In-Progress/Completed toggle button should be accordingly changed as per status of the selected project 
     1. In case for the selected favorite project, there are no line items, then in the search bar the project should be appended, and a place holder image should be shown in the screen like this sample image:  
  

     2. Please note that the toggle button in this case will be by default in "In-Progress" state 
  2. Once the user removed the selected project from the search bar, then all the results should be shown to the user

### Description

Real Time Welding Parameters.  
  
The Authorization to be followed as below:  
S.No.  | Session Name  | Path  | Authorization   
---|---|---|---  
1  | Maintain Customer Authorization | Mega Menu-> Marketing-> Authorization -> Maintain Customer Authorisation  | Marketing-2/3   
2  | Real time Welding Parameters  | Mega Menu-> Welding-> In Process Inspection-> Real Time Welding Parameters  | Welding-1/2/3, Customer type-1,2   
Mega Menu-> Production-> In Process Inspection-> Real Time Welding Parameters  | Manufacturing-1/2/3   
Mega Menu-> Quality Control-> In Process Inspection-> Real Time Welding Parameters  | Quality Control-1/2/3   
3  | Display Log Records  | Mega Menu-> Welding-> In Process Inspection-> Real Time Welding Parameter Access Log  | Welding-1/2/3   
Mega Menu-> Production-> In Process Inspection-> Real Time Welding Parameter Access Log  | Manufacturing-1/2/3   
Mega Menu-> Quality Control-> In Process Inspection-> Real Time Welding Parameter Access Log  | Quality Control-1/2/3

Please refer attach document for detailed explanation

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-18874 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\QA_QC_NDE\Feature Team 2 |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0\Sprint 18 |
| Changed Date | 2024-09-16T08:03:33.96Z |
| Created Date | 2024-06-19T06:11:59.193Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/18874) |

### Description

COPQ-Observation Points

### Acceptance Criteria

Functional: 
  1. Cosmetic Change: In case of Minor NCR, the "+" symbol should be hidden 
  2. Cosmetic Change: The COPQ values, fetched from Master in case of Minor NCR, will not be allowed to modify 
  3. Cosmetic Change: COPQ value calculated from parameters should not be allowed to be modified in the COPQ box .  If a user wants to, make change in the COPQ values, then the user needs to maintain via parameters and then system calculated value should only be visible. 
  4. CR: When the COPQ value is already calculated, and if the user clicks on the "+" symbol for editing it, then in the maintain parameter screen, the previous values should be shown in editable format 
  5. CR: For minor NCR COPQ value fetched from master should be converted into lakhs and shown in MVC page. 
  6. In COPQ filter for 'NCR Category' instead of NCR Category code description of category should be shown. 
  7.

## US-19015 — TE - Managing color Masters

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-19015 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Vishal Mishra |
| Area Path | ECOM |
| Iteration Path | ECOM\1-8-2024-15-8-2024 |
| Changed Date | 2024-08-29T04:31:17.047Z |
| Created Date | 2024-08-07T10:32:38.483Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/19015) |

### Description

As a Security Admin, I want to manage the Color Master:

### Acceptance Criteria

User find the master name on the left-top side of the body.  

  

User click the “Search” button to find existing records.  

  

User use the “New” button to add new master records.  

  

User edit existing masters by selecting them and clicking the “Edit” button.  

  

To delete an existing master, I select the record and click the “Delete” button.  

  

Note: User remember that each day of the week corresponds to a different color, and I shouldn’t use the same color for two days."

## US-19016 — TE - Managing contractor Masters

### Description

As a Security Admin, I want to manage the Contractor Master:

### Acceptance Criteria

"User can search, add, edit, and delete contractors.  

  

To add a new contractor, I click the “Add” button. A popup appears where I can enter the contractor’s name and save it. After saving, the contractor becomes visible in the master.  

  

To delete an existing contractor, I select the record and confirm deletion in the popup."

### Description

As a Security Admin, I want to manage the Role Master:

### Acceptance Criteria

"In this master table, I see columns for Full Name, Login/Username, Roles, Last Login, Active status, Web service user, and Local.  

  

To add a new user, I click the “New Local User” button. A “New Account” popup opens, allowing me to enter details like Full Name, User Name, User Role (Security, SuperAdmin, Administrator), Blocked or Active status, Language, Time Zone, New Password, and Confirm Password. I save the changes.  

  

To delete an existing user, I select the record and confirm deletion in the popup."

### Description

As a security, I want Token entry details on the homepage.

### Acceptance Criteria

Users should be able to see the L&T logo, username, logout button, and Portal name on the homepage.  

  

Users should able to see pending, closed, and all token entry details.  

  

Users should also have the option to Issue tokens, Add contractors, search, deposit tokens, and Export to Excel functions on the homepage."

As a security, I want to issue a token entry.

### Acceptance Criteria

Users should click on the “Issue Token” button to open a “Token Request” form to enter ""Entered person details.  

  

Users fill the required fields like Aadhar Number, Contractor Name, Issue Time, Token Number, Day, Token Colour, Safety Status and remark.  

  

User click “Issue” to issue the token after filling all the details.The issued tokens appear in the pending request tab.  

"

### Acceptance Criteria

User double-click on the pending token or select it and click the “Deposit Token” button then the “Token Request” popup opens with token details.  

  

User should enter the deposit time and click “Close"" Then the token status updates to closed, moving from the pending tab to the closed tab."

| Changed Date | 2024-10-18T07:10:56.39Z |
| Created Date | 2024-08-21T06:04:02.417Z |

| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/19146) |

### Description

Branch name:  
Work Item Id:  
  

  * New fields in DES051 and DES059 are already added by me. Script for your reference. 
ALTER TABLE DES051 ADD AllowCustomizeRevNo BIT DEFAULT(0) 
ALTER TABLE DES059 ADD CustomizeRevNo NVARCHAR(20) 
  

  * There will be 2 document revision fields shall be in screen and it willl display based on new field  DES051(AllowCustomizeRevNo) 
    * If DES051(AllowCustomizeRevNo) is 1 then, Document Revision field shall be invisible for user and new field DES059 (CustomizeRevNo) shall be visible for user. 
    * If DES051(AllowCustomizeRevNo) is 0 then, Document Revision field shall be visible for user and new field DES059 (CustomizeRevNo) shall be invisible for user. 
    *   



\- Both fields needs to be saved while creating the document. 
  * \- CustomizeRevNo field shall be editable for user to maintain while document is in Draft mode.

_Not provided._

## US-19377 — Cookie Consent - Implementation 

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-19377 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0\Sprint 16 |
| Changed Date | 2024-10-18T07:10:56.39Z |
| Created Date | 2024-09-07T06:23:52.317Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/19377) |

### Description

We require to develop this type of popup where user can provide their consent for store cookie in local system.

### Acceptance Criteria

Below screenshot is the sample. Actual design will prepare on later stage. 
  1. User consent will save in database and available to view for admin user. 
  2. This popup shall be displayed on login page as our default page is login. 
  3. User shall not be able to login into system without provide cookie consent as we are creating a cookie after authenticating the user into system for store user details. 
  4. If user already provided the consent, then popup will not appear on the screen for that user. 
  5. Need to develop a new static web page where user can see/read the cookie policy which release by HEIC for IEMQS. 
  6. Also make sure all critical information of user will be stored in encrypted mode in cookie.

## US-19652 — Maintain actual consumable issue using welding token UI from IEMQS

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-19652 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | TRISHUL TANDEL |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0\Sprint 16 |
| Changed Date | 2024-10-18T07:10:56.39Z |
| Created Date | 2024-10-18T06:54:10.667Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/19652) |

### Description

Maintain Issue History for KSA projects which will created from IEMQS. 
  

  

Put new button "Issue History" for view issues against token generated from IEMQS on below page. 
<https://vhzdigiiemqsqa2.lthed.com:447/IPI/WelderPSlipCopy/ConsumableSlipDetail>  

  

  

  

System will display list of issue history from DB table **WPS027  **
  

Below sample design needs to be followed for maintain issue history for KSA project.

### Acceptance Criteria

1. New button shall be applicable for all token, but button shall be displayed after actual token generated. it will not display for Draft token. 
  2. clicking on this new button system will open popup where user can see existing issue history if already have against this token. 
  3. Add token section shall be visible for those projects which are created from IEMQS. 
  4. if project is not created in IEMQS then system will not show add section to user. only listing will be available for them. 
  5. Token sequence shall be auto generated based on last issue added and this field shall be read only. system generated incremental field. 
  6. Welder details shall be displayed as read only mode. 
  7. Other fields are maintained by user. 
  8. One confirmation message ("Are you sure want to add this issue?") will appear before save data as data will not update after saving. 
  9. Once data save, below listing will refresh will latest issue history data. 
  10. Shop and Station master data shall be fetched from global m aster based on category defined. 
  11. Issue date shall have default date of Today, user can change if require. 
  12. Issue by have PS number of logged in user, user can change if require.

## US-19865 — Print Item QR Code

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-19865 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | TRISHUL TANDEL |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0\Sprint 16 |
| Changed Date | 2024-12-13T11:26:36.773Z |
| Created Date | 2024-12-12T11:51:36.727Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/19865) |

### Description

Require a QR Code icon on supply item "Tracking of Items" list from where user can print QR code of items with require quantity and size. 
  

Below options of size needs to be displayed: 
  * 40mm X 40mm
  * 50mm X 50mm
  * 60mm X 60mm
  * 70mmX70mm
  * 80mmX80mm
  * 90mmX 90mm
  * 100mm x100mm

### Acceptance Criteria

1. This popup shall be open from supply list. 
  2. This popup shall be open after receive item process. 
  3. Part no and description will display as read only mode. 
  4. Size shall be selected by user. option shall be pre-configured. 
  5. Quantity shall be entered by user. it will be mandatory and more than 0.

## US-19867 — Stuff Items Popup

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-19867 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | TRISHUL TANDEL |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0\Sprint 16 |
| Changed Date | 2024-12-18T06:42:51.563Z |
| Created Date | 2024-12-13T10:29:34.473Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/19867) |

### Description

Below popup needs to be implemented for stuff items into package. 
  

First user will select or scan package QR code to get package information as below.  This package information will display in read only mode. 
User will scan QR code of items or select from part no from supply list and add into package. 
User can add comment if required. 
User can upload/capture photos.

### Acceptance Criteria

1. Package details will fetch using manual selection of package number or scan QR code. all package details shall be displayed in read only mode. 
  2. Item shall be added using manual select part no or scan item QR code. Part description shall be auto fetched and display in read only mode. Quantity field shall be manual enter, mandatory, only numeric value and more than 0 quantity allowed. Quantity shall not be more than defined quantity of packing list. 
  3. Clicking upon QR code image, system will open the camera and allow to scan any QR code. After scanning QR code system will fetch data from system. 
  4. While scanning QR code or manual entry system will check the part is applicable to stuff in this package or not based on packing list. 
  5. Comment field shall be non-mandatory and no more than 500 characters allow. 
  6. User shall be able to attach files or capture photo using camera attached.  Photos shall be non-mandatory. 
  7. Clicking upon "STUFF" action button, confirmation message shall be displayed "Are you sure want to stuff items into package?" with Yes/No option. 
     1. if yes, data shall be captured in system and display success or failure message. 
     2. if no, confirmation message shall hide and stay on "Stuff Items" popup. 
  8. Clicking upon "CANCEL" action button, hide "Stuff Items" popup without any action perform.

## US-19939 — Paint ARM initiation

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-19939 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Pralhadsinh Rajpurohit |
| Area Path | ECOM\Paint ARM Flow Team |
| Iteration Path | ECOM |
| Changed Date | 2025-01-07T09:42:10.657Z |
| Created Date | 2025-01-07T09:42:10.657Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/19939) |

### Description

As a DCC user, I should be able to initiate the Paint ARM flow preperation so that other departments can prepare their dependent procedure and CRS and finish the procurement.

### Acceptance Criteria

An option should be available to initiate the paint ARM. 
Users should be able to select the project number. 
System should fetch the respective project number from the IEMQS system. 
**DCC** : Users should be able to upload the initial paint ARM. 
**MCC** : Users should be able to validate the paint ARM uploaded by DCC and provide appropriate comments as "Accepted" or "Commented" along with document upload. 
**Surface treatment:** Prepare and upload Paint Procedure in the system. 
**MCC:**  Users should be able to validate the paint procedure uploaded by surface treatment and provide appropriate comments as "Accepted" or "Commented" along with document upload.
**Surface Treatment** : Maintain and upload CRS 
**MCC** : Accept or comment and then Initiate procurement request to production.
**PMG** : Users can have access to all the module of the application.
Once the Flow is created then it should be displayed and maintained at the display gri of the same screen.
Export facility as well as filter activity should be vilable.

### Description

L&T sign & stamp on report

### Acceptance Criteria

1. Add ‘Offer with L&T sign & stamp’ field in pop up of Annexure-II, MT Report, PT Report. Default value ‘No’. 
  2. When ‘Offer with L&T sign & stamp’ field value is ‘Yes’, then system should add ‘Offered By’ user sign image in L&T L-II block. 
  3. When ‘Offer with L&T sign & stamp’ field value is ‘No’, then ‘This is a system generated report, L&T signature is not required.’ shall be added in L&T L-II block.

## US-23177 — L&T Certificate e-sign

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-23177 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Harsh Adadhiyawala |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\New Enhancement |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2025-12-08T12:05:08.203Z |
| Created Date | 2025-10-17T05:17:00.923Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/23177) |

### Description

Only certain authorized users (those with the QI-1 role) can electronically sign certificates, and the option to sign is hidden for everyone else. When printing a certificate, images are left out to keep things simple. The electronic signature is only added to the second page of certificates—if there’s only one page, signing isn’t allowed. The signing screen shows only the necessary fields, and the signature itself includes the date and the name of the person signing. This setup helps make the signing process secure, clear, and easy for everyone to use.

### Acceptance Criteria

* **Role-Based Access:**  
Only users with the QI-1 role can electronically sign the L&T Certificate. For all other users, the “Sign & Approve” button is hidden, and any attempt to sign via direct API or backend calls is blocked with an unauthorized error. 
* **Print Functionality:**  
Any user can print certificates from both the Index (list) and Detail (single certificate) pages. The printed version must exclude all images and only show text and data. 
* **e-Sign Window Display:**  
When a QI-1 user initiates signing, the e-sign window must not display the following fields: Witnessed/Approved by, Agency Name, TPI Name, and Intervention Stage. Only the essential fields required for signing should be visible. 
* **Page Requirement for Signing:**  
The system must allow e-signing only if the certificate has at least two pages. If a certificate has only one page, the system must prevent signing and display a clear error message explaining that single-page certificates cannot be signed. 
* **Signature Placement:**  
The electronic signature and any related text must appear only on Page 2 of the certificate. Page 1 and any other pages must not contain any signature marks or related text. 
* **Signature Format:**  
The signature must follow this exact format: “e-Signed on [system date] by [display name]”. The date must use the system’s configured format (for example, DD-MM-YYYY), and the name must match the logged-in user’s display name.

## US-23224 — Product wise checklist master (Phase 1, 2 and 3)

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-23224 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | TRISHUL TANDEL |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\New Enhancement |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2025-10-24T04:12:09.163Z |
| Created Date | 2025-10-23T06:29:37.803Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/23224) |

### Description

**As a** Planner Manager (PLNG2 Role),  

**I want** to create and manage a checklist master data,  
**So that** I can maintain product wise master checklist in system.  

  

Refer layout design: 
  

**Index Page:**
  

  

**Detail Page:**

### Acceptance Criteria

**_Form Fields:_**
> **Header Section:**
    * Product (selection option fetch from global master data with category - "Product Category") 
      * Date type: NVARCHAR(15)  

      * Duplicate product shall not be allowed to be added. 


> **Line Section:**
    * The lien table should include fields as below: 
      * Phase 
        * Dropdown selection, Mandatory
          * 1 - Full Kit for KOM 
          * 2 - Full Kit for MKOM 
          * 3 - Full Kit for Integration 
        * Date type: Integer 
      * Activity 
        * Alphanumeric value, Mandatory, Length validation 1000 characters 
        * Date type: NVARCHAR(MAX) 
      * Outcome 
        * Alphanumeric value, Mandatory, Length validation 1000 characters 
        * Date type: NVARCHAR(MAX) 
      * Preparation Time (in hrs.) 
        * Numeric value, Mandatory, Default value 0 
        * Date type: Integer 
      * Duration (in days) 
        * Numeric value, Mandatory, Default value 0 
        * Date type: Integer 


**_Create Functionality:_**
  * Users should be able to input data and save it to the master data. 


**_Edit Functionality:_**
  * Users should be able to search and edit existing master data records. 


**_Validation:_**
  * Field-level validation should be implemented (e.g., required fields, data type checks). 
  * All mandatory fields should be clearly marked and validated.  



**_Security:_**
  * Only authorized users (role PLNG2) should be able to access and modify the master data form. 


**_Audit Trail:_**
  * Changes to master data should be logged with timestamps and user information. 
  * Default fields shall be added in dB table as below for audit purpose. 
    * CreatedBy (NVARCHAR(9)) 
    * CreatedOn (Datetime) 
    * EditedBy (NVARCHAR(9))
    * EditedOn (Datetime)

## US-23235 — Create and Manage Planning Checklist for Projects

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-23235 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | TRISHUL TANDEL |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\New Enhancement |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2025-10-24T06:18:03.75Z |
| Created Date | 2025-10-24T06:14:42.913Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/23235) |

### Description

**As a** planner (PLNG3 role),  
**I want** to create and manage a planning checklist for specific projects,  
**So that** I can track activities, outcomes, and timelines efficiently throughout the project lifecycle.

### Acceptance Criteria

#### **Index Page Layout**
  

  * Displays a list of planning checklists with the following columns: 
    * **Product**
    * **Last Modified By**
    * **Last Modified On**
    * **Action** (Edit/Delete icons) 


  

#### **Detailed Page Layout**
**  
**
  * **Checklist Header Section** : 
    * **Header Id** : Auto-generated, numeric, incremented. 
    * **Project** : Dropdown selection from project master data. 
    * **Product** : Dropdown selection from global master data filtered by "Product Category". 
    * **Customer** : Auto-fetched based on selected project, read-only. 
    * **Planner** : Auto-fetched PS number and name of logged-in user, read-only. 
    * **LOI Date** : Auto-fetched from project master data, read-only. 
    * **Integration Date** : User-selectable date. 
    * **CDD** : Auto-fetched from project master data, read-only. 
    * **Production Centre** : Dropdown selection from master data. 
  * **Checklist Line Items** : 
    * Upon clicking **Create** , system fetches master checklist based on selected product and maps it to the selected project. 
    * Auto-maintained line table includes: 
      * **Line Id** : Auto-generated, numeric, incremented. 
      * **Header Id** : Reference to header. 
      * **Phase No**
      * **Sr. No.**
      * **Activity**
      * **Outcome**
      * **Planned Completion Date** : 
        * Calculated based on LOI date for Phase 1 & 2\. 
        * Calculated based on Integration date for Phase 3. 
      * **Actual Completion Date**
      * **Remarks**
      * **Status** : Dropdown with values: 
        * NS (Not Started) 
        * IP (In Progress) 
        * CO (Completed) 
      * **Action** : 
        * **Edit** : Allowed only if status is NS. 
        * **Delete** : Allowed only if status is NS. 
      * **Timeline** : Captures all user actions on the specific activity.

## US-23322 — Digitize ASME Inspection Checklist for Authorized Inspector Signature

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-23322 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\New Enhancement |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2025-10-29T00:01:14.103Z |
| Created Date | 2025-10-29T00:01:14.103Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/23322) |

### Description

#### **Title:  **Digitize ASME Inspection Checklist for Authorized Inspector Signature 
* * *
#### **As a:**
Quality Control Engineer (L&T QC)  
Authorized Inspector (ASME AI) 
* * *
#### **I want to:**
Prepare, update, and manage ASME inspection checklists digitally, and offer them to the Authorized Inspector for electronic signature after completion of inspection activities. 
* * *
#### **So that I can:**
  * Eliminate manual paperwork and physical signatures. 
  * Ensure compliance with ASME standards. 
  * Reduce delays caused by in-person approvals. 
  * Maintain accurate, traceable records for audits and regulatory requirements. 


* * *
### **Detailed Description**
Currently, ASME inspection checklists are prepared in Excel and signed physically by the Authorized Inspector after inspection activities. This process is time-consuming, repetitive, and dependent on physical presence. The new digital solution will: 
  * Provide a **web-based interface** under **Menu → Quality Control → Advance Quality Planning**. 
  * Introduce two new sections: 
    1. **Maintain ASME Inspection Checklist** – Create and edit checklists. 
    2. **ASME Inspection Master List** – View all checklists with filters and search. 


* * *
### **Acceptance Criteria**
  1. **Checklist Creation**
     * User can create a new checklist using a predefined template. 
     * Checklist includes fields: 
       * Sr. No. 
       * Section/Assembly 
       * Description 
       * AI Check Point Type (Dropdown: H, W, RW, R, NA) 
       * Description of Check Points 
       * Acceptance Criteria / Code Reference 
       * L&T QC Status (Pending/Complete) 
       * L&T QC Sign & Date (Auto-filled on sign) 
       * ASME AI Status (Pending/Complete) 
       * ASME AI Sign & Date (Auto-filled on sign) 
       * Comments/Remarks 
       * Attachments (File upload) 
  2. **Dynamic Row Management**
     * Ability to add, edit, and delete rows dynamically. 
     * Auto-renumber Sr. No. when rows are added or deleted. 
  3. **Digital Signature**
     * Clicking “Sign” auto-fills username and timestamp. 
     * Separate sign buttons for L&T QC and ASME AI. 
  4. **Status Tracking**
     * Dropdown for Pending/Complete for both L&T QC and AI. 
     * Visual indicators for status. 
  5. **Attachments**
     * Upload supporting documents per checklist item. 
  6. **Master List**
     * View all checklists with filters (Project, Equipment, Status, Date). 
     * Search functionality. 
  7. **Notifications**
     * Email alerts to AI when L&T QC completes stages. 
     * Reminders for pending actions. 


* * *
### **Benefits**
  * **Efficiency:** Faster turnaround for inspection approvals. 
  * **Compliance:** Adherence to ASME standards with digital traceability. 
  * **Transparency:** Real-time status updates for all stakeholders. 
  * **Data Security:** Secure storage of signed documents and attachments.

### Acceptance Criteria

#### **1\. Checklist Creation**
  * User can create a new checklist under **Menu → Quality Control → Advance Quality Planning → Maintain ASME Inspection Checklist**. 
  * Checklist includes the following fields: 
    * **Sr. No.** (Auto-generated and re-sequenced when rows are added/deleted) 
    * **Section/Assembly** (Text input) 
    * **Description** (Text input) 
    * **AI Check Point Type** (Dropdown: H – Hold, W – Witness, RW – Random Witness, R – Review, NA – Not Applicable) 
    * **Description of Check Points** (Text input) 
    * **Acceptance Criteria / Code Reference** (Text input) 
    * **L &T QC Status** (Dropdown: Pending/Complete) 
    * **L &T QC Sign & Date** (Auto-filled when “Sign” button clicked) 
    * **ASME AI Status** (Dropdown: Pending/Complete) 
    * **ASME AI Sign & Date** (Auto-filled when “Sign” button clicked) 
    * **Comments/Remarks** (Textarea) 
    * **Attachments** (File upload) 


* * *
#### **2\. Dynamic Row Management**
  * Ability to **Add Row** dynamically with all fields editable. 
  * Ability to **Delete Selected Rows** using checkboxes. 
  * Auto-renumber **Sr. No.** after deletion or addition. 


* * *
#### **3\. Digital Signature**
  * Clicking **Sign** button auto-fills: 
    * Logged-in username. 
    * Current system date and time. 
  * Signature button disappears after signing to prevent duplicate signing. 


* * *
#### **4\. Status Tracking**
  * Dropdown for **Pending/Complete** for both L&T QC and ASME AI. 
  * Visual indicator (color or icon) for status changes. 


* * *
#### **5\. Attachments**
  * File upload supported for each checklist row. 
  * Multiple file types allowed (PDF, JPG, PNG, DOCX). 
  * Preview or download option for uploaded files. 


* * *
#### **6\. Comments**
  * Free-text field for remarks per checklist item. 
  * Minimum 200 characters allowed for detailed notes. 


* * *
#### **7\. Master List**
  * Displays all created checklists in a tabular view. 
  * Filters available for: 
    * Project Name 
    * Equipment 
    * Status (Pending/Complete) 
    * Date Range 
  * Search functionality by keyword. 


* * *
#### **8\. Notifications**
  * Email notification sent to AI when L&T QC completes all stages and submits checklist. 
  * Reminder notifications for pending signatures. 


* * *
#### **9\. Security & Access**
  * Role-based access: 
    * **L &T QC:** Create, edit, sign, submit. 
    * **ASME AI:** Review, sign only. 
    * **Admin:** Full access. 
  * All actions logged for audit trail. 


* * *
#### **10\. Performance**
  * System should support at least **100 concurrent users** without performance degradation. 
  * Checklist load time should be **< 3 seconds**.

### Description

Regarding EMS to DFS data updates. At present there is a provision in EMS to define PBU and Mfg.Location at header level as well as Line item wise, based on PBU defined at Line Item wise, DFS are generated for each PBU, but the location is not passed properly for each line item, but it passed from a header. As a result, though the enquiry have line items with different locations, they are not visible to respective locations accordingly. Kindly solve the bug and make sure while creating new enquiry as well as on any change or revision, appropriate location should be passed for each line item to DFS, so that DFS get affected duly. Also, after making this change, we want to have one walkthrough session with extensive testing to avoid any future hiccups as it is a critical system. For above problem - Vimal Upadhyay (Digital Initiatives - HE HZW) is fully aware about requirements.

### Acceptance Criteria

Application - Order Inflow & Enquiry Management Session - Load / Capacity Enquiry Update ERER No. - E04250148 Complete updating log required for the above given enquiry in session motioned above from date 8th Aug 2025 till date. Plz call on 9619493997 / extn 7546 for any query.

### Description

As a **QC2 user** , I want the ability to add a new stage to multiple Position Numbers at the same time so that I don’t have to update each Position Number individually.

### Acceptance Criteria

*   

  * A new button **“Add Stages”** should be available next to the **Export** button. 
  * On clicking **“Add Stages”** , a popup window should open. 
  * The popup must allow: 
    * Selecting multiple Position Numbers. 
    * Selecting a stage to add. 
  * On clicking **Add** , the selected stage should be added to **all selected Position Numbers**. 
  * The system must show a confirmation or success message after completion.

### Description

As a **QC2 user** , I want to copy an existing Part ICL from one Position Number to other Position Numbers within the same project so that I can quickly replicate ICL data.

### Acceptance Criteria

* A **Copy** option should be available for Position-to-Position (P2P) copying. 
  * On clicking **Copy** , a popup must open. 
  * The “Copy To Position” list should show only Position Numbers where ICL has **not** yet been generated. 
  * On confirming: 
    * Quality ID, ICL stages, and protocol should be copied to all selected Position Numbers. 
  * If Position Number already has ICL data, the system **must not** copy and should skip it.

### Description

******Modify the WPS Welding Parameter section in both the UI and the report print layout for Overlay Joint and the following welding processes: ESW, FCAW, GTAW, HW GTAW, GMAW, and GTAW-P.**  

  

As a Welding Engineer/User(Role- WE3)
I want the WPS Welding Parameter section to show updated labels for specific welding processes  
So that the welding data is displayed correctly as per revised standards.  

  

  

  

On clicking **Add Line** in the Welding Parameter section: 
  * A **new popup** must open. 
  * The popup must allow maintaining welding parameters exactly as per provided screenshots.
  * Change in Print  For process **ESW, FCAW, GTAW, HW GTAW, GMAW, GTAW-P** as below SS****
  *     Change in Print  For process **SMAW** as below SS

    * UI 
    * Print 
  * Only for: 
    * Overlay and Hard-Facing WPS 
    * Created **after 2024-01-01**
  * No change in WPS created before this date.

## US-23462 — CTQ Module Shop Flow

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-23462 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Praksah Vasaikar |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\New Enhancement |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2025-11-06T08:23:51.267Z |
| Created Date | 2025-11-06T07:31:33.447Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/23462) |

### Description

**1\. Add Production Center & Update Name of Process **  

  * As a QC2 user, I want to add Production Center information and remove unnecessary fields in the Name of Process Maser, so that the CTQ creation process is aligned with updated business requirements.


  

  

**2\. Show Annexure Popup on CTQ Submit**  

  * As a QC user, I want a popup to appear after clicking the Submit CTQ button
  * As a **system** , I must save each process-wise CTQ entry so that the data can be routed to respective shops.  

  *   * As a **QC user** , I want previously selected shop and in-charge details from CTQ to auto-fill in the popup so that I don’t re-enter the same information.  



**3\. Send CTQ to All Selected Shop In-Charges**  

  * As a QC user, I want each applicable shop in-charge to receive the CTQ task so that they can acknowledge it. 


**4.Shop Acknowledgement Flow**  

  * As a Shop In-Charge, I want to acknowledge assigned CTQs so that QC can approve only after all shops have reviewed.


**  
**
**5.Send Email Notification on Acknowledgement**  

  * As a CTQ initiator, I want to receive an automated email when a shop acknowledges the CTQ so that I remain informed of progress.

### Acceptance Criteria

**1\. Add Production Center & Update Name of Process**  

  * Remove Select Department from Name of Process screen. 
  * Add Select Production Center dropdown. 
    * Data to be fetched from Global Master (Category: CTQ Production Centre). 
  * Function dropdown to be fetched from Global Master (Category: CTQ Function). 
  * In Maintain CTQ → Add CTQ: 
    * Add Production Center field before Function. 
    * Save in database table CTQ002 (new column `ProductionCenter nvarchar(40)`). 


**2\. Show Annexure Popup on CTQ Submit**  

  * On clicking Submit, a popup will open showing required CTQ details as per Annexure-1. 
  * Popup includes 6 fields: 
    * Process 
    * Applicable (Yes/No) 
    * Detail of CTQ 
    * Production Centre 
    * Shop 
    * Shop In-Charge 
  * Processwise CTQ Details should be stored in new table 
  * If Applicable = Yes: 
    * Detail of CTQ must be **alphanumeric** , **200 characters** , **mandatory**. 
  * If Applicable = No: 
    * CTQ Detail auto-filled as **NA** (non-editable). 
  * Applicable Shop: 
    * Dropdown, data fetched from **Name of Process Master** , **mandatory**. 
  * Applicable Shop In-Charge: 
    * Dropdown, dependent on selected Shop, **mandatory**. 
  *   * System fetches selected Process, Shop, and In-Charge from CTQ. 
  * Other process rows remain blank for user input. 


**3\. Send CTQ to All Selected Shop In-Charges**  

  * After Submit CTQ: 
    * CTQ data goes to all selected Shop In-Charge trays. 
  * For Applicable = No: 
    * System still sends to shop with message:  
"There is no specific CTQ identified for this project related to XXXX shop."

**4.Shop Acknowledgement Flow**  

  * In-Charge opens Maintain CTQ → clicks Acknowledge. 
  * Status for that shop updates to Complete. 
  * Only after all shops acknowledge, QC2 can approve. 
  * Shop Name must be displayed in CTQ Header. 

**5.Send Email Notification on Acknowledgement**  

After each shop acknowledges: 
  * Email sent to initiator stating:  
"XXX shop has reviewed & acknowledged project CTQ."

## US-23470 — Maintain Master Data for Resource Allocation

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-23470 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 1 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\New Enhancement |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2025-11-28T06:35:21.437Z |
| Created Date | 2025-11-06T11:02:06.003Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/23470) |

### Description

****
**As a** Production Planner  
**I want** to maintain a master data table that maps ID/Blank and Length ranges to specific resources  
**So that** I can automatically assign the correct machine (Lathe, VTL-West, K-VTL, SKZ) based on part dimensions.

### Acceptance Criteria

* **Create & Update Records**
*   * Ability to add or edit rules with non-mandatory fields: 
    * **ID/Blank** (numeric or range) 
    * **Length** (numeric or range) 
    * **Resource** (text) 
  * Predefined mapping: 
    * `<200` → Lathe 
    * `200-500` & `<1000` → VTL-West 
    * `200-500` & `>1000` → K-VTL 
    * `>500` → SKZ 


* ****
ID/Blank |  Length |  Resource  
---|---|---  
<200 |  - |  Lathe  
200-500 |  <1000 |  VTL-West  
200-500 |  >1000 |  K-VTL  
>500 |  - |  SKZ  
**  
**
* **Validation**
  * Ensure ranges do not overlap incorrectly. 
  * Resource must be selected from predefined list. 


* **Auto-Assignment**
  * When a new part is entered with ID and Length, system suggests the correct resource based on rules. 


* **View & Search**
  * Ability to view all mapping rules in a tabular format. 
  * Ability to filter by Resource or range. 


* **Export**
  * Ability to export master data to Excel for reporting.

## US-23472 — Performance Optimization

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-23472 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\New Enhancement |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2025-11-27T06:40:36.847Z |
| Created Date | 2025-11-06T11:06:23.14Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/23472) |

### Description

As a user  
I want faster data entry, updates, and status changes  
So that I can work efficiently without delays or performance issues.

### Acceptance Criteria

* Optimize Data Entry 
  * Improve backend performance for adding new items. 
  * Reduce query execution time for insert operations. 


* Reduce Delays in Status Updates 
  * Optimize processes for: 
    * Nozzle Status Updates (IMR Status, Seam Status). 
    * CCPM Data Update. 
    * Weld Kg Update. 
  * Ensure updates reflect in UI within 2 seconds of submission. 


* Remove Unnecessary Linkages 
  * Identify and eliminate unused columns or redundant joins in queries. 
  * Simplify data models where possible without breaking functionality. 


* Implement Caching 
  * Cache frequently accessed data (e.g., master lists, static configurations). 
  * Refresh cache on data change or at defined intervals. 


* UI Responsiveness 
  * Optimize rendering for grids and tables. 
  * Reduce heavy scripts and unnecessary DOM elements.

So that the overlay plan grid reflects updated terminology and provides additional scheduling details.

### Acceptance Criteria

    * WP for ID → ID Station 
    * WP for Face → GF Station

    * WP for Backface → BF Station

## US-23477 — Calculate and Display Start Date & End Date for Components

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-23477 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\New Enhancement |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2025-11-27T05:19:05.23Z |
| Created Date | 2025-11-06T11:21:35.093Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/23477) |

### Description

As a user  
I want the system to calculate Start Date, End Date, and Cycle Time based on welding logic  
So that I can plan component timelines accurately and update them when needed.

### Acceptance Criteria

* Add Columns 
  * Add Start Date, End Date, and Cycle Time columns in the relevant session. 


* Calculation Logic 
  * Start Date = Previous Component’s Start Date + (Previous Component’s ID Weld KG ÷ Weld Deposition per day) 
  * Cycle Time = Attached File 
  * End Date = Start Date + Cycle Time 


* Dynamic Update 
  * When the user clicks Update Date, the system recalculates Start Date, End Date, and Cycle Time dynamically based on the latest data. 


* Editable Fields 
  * Start Date, End Date, and Cycle Time columns should be editable by the user. 
  * If edited, system should log changes for audit purposes. 


* UI Behavior 
  * Display calculated dates in respective columns. 
  * Provide an Update Date button to trigger recalculation. 
  * Ensure validation for date formats and logical sequence (Start Date < End Date).

## US-23478 — Integration with Procurement System

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-23478 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\New Enhancement |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2025-11-27T04:37:42.82Z |
| Created Date | 2025-11-06T11:22:36.417Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/23478) |

### Description

As a user  
I want to select a nozzle procurement UID from a dropdown and view its planned receipt date  
So that I can quickly access procurement timelines without manual lookup.

### Acceptance Criteria

* Dropdown for UID Selection 
  * A dropdown should display all available Nozzle Procurement UIDs. 
  * Data for UIDs should be fetched from Concerto or an internal source. 


* Fetch Planned Receipt Date 
  * When a UID is selected, the system should:
    * Call the Concerto API. 
    * Retrieve the Planned Receipt Date for the selected UID. 
  * Display the Planned receipt datein the UI. 


* UI Behavior 
  * Dropdown should have a search/filter option for quick UID selection. 
  * Display field should update dynamically when a new UID is selected. 
  * Handle API errors gracefully (e.g., show “Data not available” message). 


* Data Handling 
  * API integration should follow secure authentication. 
  * Ensure response parsing for Planned Receipt Date is accurate. 
  * Nozzle UID and date should not be cached beyond session unless specified.

## US-23479 — Enable Column Customization and Save Preferred Layout in Display Overlay Plan Session

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-23479 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\New Enhancement |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2025-11-27T04:21:59.567Z |
| Created Date | 2025-11-06T11:23:58.327Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/23479) |

### Description

As an User   
I want to customize the table view by hiding unnecessary columns and saving my preferred layout  
So that I can view only relevant data and maintain a consistent layout across sessions.

### Acceptance Criteria

1. Column Customization 
     * A Settings (gear icon) is available in the Display Overlay Plan session. 
     * Clicking the gear icon opens a popup window listing all available columns with checkboxes for selection. 
     * Users can hide or show columns by checking/unchecking boxes. 
  2. Save User-Specific Layout 
     * Popup includes Save, Cancel, and Reset to Default buttons. 
     * When the user clicks Save, the system: 
       * Stores the selected columns in a configuration table linked to the user profile. 
       * Applies the saved layout immediately. 
     * On subsequent logins, the saved layout is applied by default. 
  3. Scope 
     * Customization applies only to the Display Overlay Plan session. 
     * Does not affect other sessions like Maintain Component Overlay. 
  4. UI Behavior 
     * Popup should: 
       * Display all available columns with checkboxes. 
       * Include buttons: Save, Cancel, Reset to Default. 
     * After saving, the table refreshes with the selected columns. 
  5. Data Handling 
     * Store user-specific layout in a configuration table with fields: 
       * User ID 
       * Session Name 
       * Selected Columns

## US-23568 — Protocol Revision Functionality for Seam Inspection Reports

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-23568 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Praksah Vasaikar |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\New Enhancement |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2025-11-12T05:57:18.563Z |
| Created Date | 2025-11-12T05:49:04.227Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/23568) |

### Description

### Requirement :- 
### As a **QC User** ,
I want to **revise the protocol data** for specified Seam Inspection Protocols,  
so that I can update and maintain accurate protocol information with revision history for audit and traceability.  

  

  

  

  

**Scope :-**
**
****
**
The **Revise Option** must be added for the following protocols: 
**Protocol No****** |  **Protocol Description******  
---|---  
PROTOCOL036_1 |  SET-UP & DIMENSION REPORT FOR ROLLED SHELL LONGITUDINAL SEAM  
PROTOCOL043_1 |  VISUAL & DIMENSION REPORT FOR ROLLED SHELL LONGITUDINAL SEAM  
PROTOCOL037_1 |  SET-UP & DIMENSION REPORT FOR CIRCUMFERENTIAL SEAM  
PROTOCOL044_1 |  VISUAL AND DIMENSION REPORT FOR CIRCUMFERENTIAL SEAM  
PROTOCOL042_1 |  SET-UP & DIMENSION REPORT FOR NOZZLE  
PROTOCOL047_1 |  WELD VISUAL & DIMENSION REPORT FOR NOZZLE  
PROTOCOL038_1 |  SET-UP INSPECTION REPORT FOR FORMED HEMISPHERICAL D'END  
PROTOCOL045_1 |  VISUAL AND DIMENSION INSPECTION REPORT FOR FORMED HEMISPHERICAL D'END  
PROTOCOL041_1 |  SET-UP INSPECTION REPORT FOR FORMED ELLIPSODIAL,TORISPHERICAL D'END WITH CROWN AND TIER  
PROTOCOL046_1 |  VISUAL AND DIMENSION INSPECTION REPORT FOR ELLIPSOIDAL,TORISPHERICAL FORMED D'END  
PROTOCOL052_1 |  SET-UP & DIMENSION REPORT FOR PEPE TO PIPE, PIEPE TO FLANGE, PIPE TO ELBOW SEAM, PIPE TO FORGING, FORGING TO FLANGE, ELBOW TO FLANGE  
PROTOCOL053_1 |  VISUAL & DIMENSION REPORT FOR PEPE TO PIPE, PIEPE TO FLANGE, PIPE TO ELBOW SEAM, PIPE TO FORGING, FORGING TO FLANGE, ELBOW TO FLANGE  
  

Remaining protocol list will be provided later. 
**Current work flow :-**
  1. QC3 logs in → Maintain ICL → Attaches protocol against the stage 
  2. QC3 fills protocol details 
  3. QC3 submits ICL 
  4. QC2 approves ICL 
  5. Production user offers stage when protocol data is filled 
  6. QC3 Attend the same, Submit Protocol Data and Cleared the stage. 


## ****New Requirement :-****
**1\. Add Revision Functionality**
  * Add **Revision No** field in Protocol (default = **R0**) 
  * Revision No **should not appear in protocol print**
  * Add **Revise button** on Protocol page 
  * Revise button becomes visible only **after protocol submission**


**2\. Revision Flow**
  1. QC User clicks **Revise**
  2. System shows **confirmation popup**
  3. QC User enters **Revise Remark**
  4. System: 
     * Saves remark in protocol table 
     * Increments Revision No by **+1** (R1, R2, etc.) 
     * Updates **Edited By** and **Edited On**
     * Enables all QC fields for data editing 
     * Saves previous revision PDF on FCS Server 


**3\. Protocol History**
  * Add **Protocol History** button 
  * On click, show popup displaying Previous Revision Details. 
    * Revision list 
    * Revision No 
    * Revised By 
    * Revised On 
    * Remark 
  * **View button** opens corresponding PDF saved on FCS Server

### Acceptance Criteria

* Protocol must start with **Revision No = R0**


  * Revise button should be visible only after initial protocol submission. 


  * System must display popup asking for confirmation before revision. 


  * All QC fields become editable again. 
  * Revision No increments correctly. 
  * Revise Remark, Edit By, Edit On are stored. 


  * Protocol History popup must display complete revision list. 
  * View button must open correct PDF from FCS Server. 


  * Revision number must **not** appear on the print. 


  * Only QC users can revise protocols. 
  * Production users cannot revise.

  * Revision No maintained (default R0)

| NDE3 revisions require NDE2 approval

| Changed Date | 2025-12-18T06:20:58.507Z |
| Created Date | 2025-11-27T06:45:26.897Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/24154) |

### Description

As a user  
I want to delete rows from the Pending tab  
So that I can remove unnecessary or incorrect entries and keep the data clean

### Acceptance Criteria

* Delete Button 
  * Each row in the Pending tab should have a Delete button. 
  * Button should be clearly visible and accessible. 
  *   



* Confirmation Popup 
  * On clicking Delete, show a confirmation popup: 
    * Message: _“Are you sure you want to delete this record?”_
    * Buttons: Yes (proceed), No (cancel). 


* Database Update 
  * If user confirms deletion: 
    * Remove the record from the database. 
    * Ensure referential integrity (no orphaned data). 


* UI Refresh 
  * After successful deletion: 
    * Refresh the Pending tab grid. 
    * Show a success message: _“Record deleted successfully.”_


* Error Handling 
  * If deletion fails: 
    * Display an error message: _“Unable to delete record. Please try again.”_

## US-24155 — Overlay station logic based on welding category. Auto-populate fields during component creation.

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-24155 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\New Enhancement |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2025-12-18T06:20:36.993Z |
| Created Date | 2025-11-27T06:52:34.863Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/24155) |

### Description

As a user  
I want the system to automatically populate the Overlay Station field based on the selected welding category  
So that I can save time and ensure correct station allocation without manual entry.

### Acceptance Criteria

1. Overlay Station Logic 
     * When creating a new component: 
       * System checks the Welding Category (e.g., ID, GF, BF, Nub). 
       * Auto-populates the Overlay Station field using predefined mapping rules. 
     * Mapping should be configurable in the Station Mapping Master. 
     * Logic: will be in attached SOR 
  2. UI Behavior 
     * During component creation: 
       * Overlay Station field should display the auto-selected value. 
       * Field should remain editable for manual override. 
     * If user changes Welding Category, Overlay Station should update dynamically. 
  3. Data Handling 
     * Save the final Overlay Station value (auto or manually edited) in the database. 
     * Ensure audit logging for overrides. 
  4. Performance 
     * Auto-population should occur instantly without noticeable delay.

As a user  
I want a Future Load Visualization tab that shows workload projections by machine and time period  
So that I can plan resources effectively and anticipate capacity constraints.

### Acceptance Criteria

1. New Tab: Future Load Visualization 
     * Add a dedicated tab in the portal for workload projections. 
     * Display data in a graphical format (charts/graphs) for better visualization. 
  2. Visualization Details 
     * Show station loading by: 
       * Machine 
       * Time Period (e.g., monthly) 
     * Graph should represent Weld Kg deposition required per month for each station. 
  3. Filters 
     * Provide filters for: 
       * Component Types: Nozzle, Blank, Nub, Pipe, Elbow, All 
       * Customer 
       * Project No. 
       * Welding Process Category 
     * Filters should dynamically update the visualization. 
  4. UI Behavior 
     * Interactive charts (hover for details, drill-down if possible). 
     * Refresh visualization when filters are applied. 
     * Export option for graphical report (PDF/Excel). 
  5. Data Handling 
     * Fetch data from existing workload and welding deposition tables. 
     * Ensure accurate aggregation by month and station. 
     * Optimize queries for performance.

### Description

As a user  
I want the system to generate a detailed report for parts under MT (Machining Tasks) and PT (Processing Tasks)  
So that I can review all necessary details for Quality purposes.

### Acceptance Criteria

* Report Generation 
  * System should generate a report that includes: 
    * All parts categorized under MT and PT. 
    * Required details such as: 
      * Technique No. from NDE Technique No. field which will be Open field. 
      * Consumable details will be fetched from the technique no.
      * Add a Columns name “Quantity” the data will be coming from the "accepted Quantity".
      * Add Dropdown list of Examination by.
      * Part Number (similar to Seam Number in other reports).
  * Send for E-verification 


* UI Behavior 
  * Display report in a tabular format with an option to export (PDF). 


* Data Handling 
  * Fetch data from NDE Test Results. 
  * Ensure real-time accuracy (latest updates reflected in the report). 
  * Optimize query for performance.

## US-24159 — Upload Folder and Files to Project Repository

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-24159 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | DEEPIKA MEENA |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\New Enhancement |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2025-11-27T10:19:25.507Z |
| Created Date | 2025-11-27T10:10:41.67Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/24159) |

### Description

**As a** user,  
**I want** to upload an entire folder (including its subfolders and files) to the project repository,  
**So that** I can quickly add multiple files and maintain the folder structure without manually uploading each file.

### Acceptance Criteria

* **Upload Option:**
  * The UI should provide an option to upload: 
    * Individual files 
    * Entire folders 


* **Folder Structure Preservation:**
  * When a folder is uploaded, its internal structure (subfolders and files) should remain intact in the repository. 


* **Drag and Drop Support:**
  * Users should be able to drag and drop a folder into the upload area. 


* **Progress Indicator:**
  * Show upload progress for each file and folder. 


* **Error Handling:**
  * Display clear error messages for unsupported file types or upload failures. 


* **Size Limit Validation:**
  * Validate folder size against repository limits before upload. 


* **Cross-Browser Support:**
  * Ensure functionality works on major browsers (Chrome, Firefox, Edge). 


* **Security:**
  * Validate files for malicious content before saving. 


* **Confirmation:**
  * After upload, show a success message and the uploaded folder structure in the repository.

## US-24218 — Replace Help-Desk Icon with Camera Icon and Integrate Picture Capture Logic

| Field | Value |

| ID | US-24218 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\New Enhancement |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2025-12-18T06:20:29.76Z |
| Created Date | 2025-12-01T04:30:44.63Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/24218) |

### Description

**As a** user,  
**I want** the Help-Desk icon on the home screen to be replaced with a Camera icon,  
**So that** I can quickly access the camera functionality to capture pictures directly from the home screen.

### Acceptance Criteria

* **UI Change:**
  * Remove the existing Help-Desk icon from the home screen. 
  * Add a Camera icon in the same position with proper alignment and styling consistent with the app’s design system. 
  * Ensure the icon is responsive across different screen sizes and orientations. 


* **Navigation & Logic:**
  * On tapping the Camera icon: 
    * Check for camera permissions: 
      * If granted → Open the camera interface. 
      * If not granted → Prompt user with permission dialog. 
    * Integrate existing picture capture logic under this flow: 
      * Launch device camera using native API or existing module. 
      * Allow user to capture an image. 
      * Provide options: 
        * **Preview** captured image. 
        * **Retake** image. 
        * **Confirm** image. 
      * On confirmation: 
        * Save image on Camera Rolls Folder. 
        * If there is no Camera roll folder under each project, need to create a Folder name as "Camera Rolls"
    * Handle edge cases: 
      * Camera unavailable → Show error message. 
      * Permission denied → Show guidance to enable permissions.

**So that** I can capture videos directly from the same screen without navigating elsewhere.
