## US-24221 — Make Camera View Full Screen on Devices (6.5" Mobile & 7" Tablet)

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-24221 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\New Enhancement |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2025-12-18T06:20:14.04Z |
| Created Date | 2025-12-01T04:38:28.86Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/24221) |

### Description

**As a** user,  
**I want** the camera preview to occupy the full screen instead of a partial view,  
**So that** I can have a better experience while capturing photos or videos.

### Acceptance Criteria

* **Current Behavior:**
    * Camera preview is displayed in a partial area of the screen (e.g., within a container or fixed layout). 
  * **New Requirement:**
    * Camera preview should occupy **full screen** on: 
      * Mobile devices with screen size ~6.5". 
      * Tablets with screen size ~7". 
    * Remove any extra UI elements that restrict the camera view area (e.g., margins, headers, footers). 
    * Ensure buttons like **Take Photo** and **Take Video** remain accessible without overlapping the camera view. 


* * *
### **UI Changes:**
  * Full-screen camera preview: 
    * Use device width and height dynamically. 
    * Maintain aspect ratio for camera feed. 
  * Position action buttons (Take Photo, Take Video) as overlays: 
    * Bottom center or bottom bar. 
    * Ensure responsive design for both mobile and tablet. 


* * *
### **Functionality:**
  * When user opens the camera: 
    * Preview should scale to full screen. 
    * Buttons should remain functional and visible. 
  * Handle orientation changes (portrait/landscape): 
    * Camera preview should adjust accordingly. 
  * Permissions remain as per existing logic. 


### **Acceptance Criteria**
  1. Camera preview occupies full screen on supported devices. 
  2. Action buttons are visible and accessible without blocking the view. 
  3. No distortion or cropping of camera feed. 
  4. Works in both portrait and landscape modes. 
  5. No crashes or performance issues.

## US-24226 — Display New Screen After Photo/Video Capture field with Project Selection and Upload Option

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-24226 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\New Enhancement |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2025-12-18T06:20:06.29Z |
| Created Date | 2025-12-01T05:17:47.92Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/24226) |

### Description

**As a** user,  
**I want** a new screen to appear after I capture a photo or video,  
**So that** I can select a project, name the file, and upload or retake the media easily.

### Acceptance Criteria

* **Trigger:**
    * After user clicks **Take Photo** or **Take Video** and confirms the capture, navigate to a next screen. 
  * **Screen Layout:**
    * **Fields:**
      1. **Project No. (Dropdown):**
         * Fetch list of project numbers from the **Project Document Folder**. 
         * Populate dropdown dynamically. 
         * Handle empty list or API failure gracefully. 
      2. **File Name (Text Field):**
         * Allow user to enter a custom file name. 
         * Validate for: 
           * No special characters except underscore or dash. 
           * Length limit (max 100 characters). 
    * **Buttons:**
      * **Upload:**
        * Upload the captured photo/video to the selected project folder with the given file name. 
        * Show progress indicator during upload. 
        * Display success/failure message. 
      * **Retake:**
        * Navigate back to camera screen for retake. 
      * **Close:**
        * Exit the workflow for the same project and return to camera screen 1. 


### ****
### **Functionality Flow**
  1. Capture photo/video → Confirm → Navigate to next screen. 
  2. Load project list from backend or local cache. 
  3. User selects project number and enters file name. 
  4. On **Upload** : 
     * Validate inputs. 
     * Call API to upload file to corresponding project folder. 
     * Handle success/failure gracefully. 
  5. On **Retake** : 
     * Clear current media and return to camera screen. 
  6. On **Close** : 
     * Discard media and return to home screen. 


### **Technical Considerations**
  * **API Integration:**
    * Endpoint to fetch project list. 
    * Endpoint to upload media file with metadata (project no., file name). 
  * **UI/UX:**
    * Responsive design for mobile and tablet. 
    * Ensure buttons are clearly visible and accessible. 
  * **Error Handling:**
    * Network failures. 
    * Invalid file name. 
    * Empty project selection. 
  * **Performance:**
    * Optimize upload for large video files limit would for 2minutes with count down. 
    * Show progress bar for uploads. 


### **Acceptance Criteria**
  1. New screen appears after photo/video capture confirmation. 
  2. Project dropdown loads correctly from project document folder. 
  3. File name field accepts valid input. 
  4. Upload button successfully uploads media with metadata. 
  5. Retake and Close buttons work as expected. 
  6. No crashes or UI glitches on mobile (6.5") and tablet (7").

## US-24227 — Add “Move to Folder” Button to Transfer Media from Camera Roll to Department Folder

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-24227 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\New Enhancement |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2025-12-18T06:19:36.353Z |
| Created Date | 2025-12-01T05:39:43.67Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/24227) |

### Description

**As a** user (or any user within the same department),  

**I want** a **Move to Folder** button on the camera roll screen,  
**So that** I can move captured photos/videos from the camera roll to my department’s designated folder.

### Acceptance Criteria

* **Current Behavior:**
    * Users can capture photos/videos and upload them, but there is no option to move existing media from the camera roll to a department folder. 
  * **New Requirement:**
    * Add a **Move to Folder** button alongside existing options (e.g., Upload, Delete). 
    * The logic for **Move to Folder** should follow similar steps as the **Delete** button, but instead of removing the file, it transfers it to the selected department folder. 

  

### **UI Changes:**
  * On the media management screen: 
    * Display **Move to Folder** button near Add New Folder and Upload Files buttons. 
    * Ensure consistent styling and accessibility. 
  * When **Move to Folder** is clicked: 
    * Show a **folder selection dialog** : 
      * Fetch list of department folders from backend. 
      * Allow user to select the target folder. 
    * Confirm action before moving the file. 


### **Functionality Flow:**
  1. User selects media from camera roll. 
  2. Clicks **Move to Folder**. 
  3. System displays folder selection dropdown (department folders). 
  4. User selects folder and confirms. 
  5. System: 
     * Validates permissions (user belongs to department). 
     * Moves file from camera roll to selected folder. 
     * Updates metadata and file path in database. 
  6. Show success/failure message. 


### **Technical Considerations:**
  * **API Integration:**
    * Endpoint to fetch department folders. 
    * Endpoint to move file (update file location in storage and database). 
  * **Permissions:**
    * Validate user department before allowing move. 
  * **Error Handling:**
    * Network failures. 
    * Invalid folder selection. 
    * File not found. 
  * **Performance:**
    * Optimize for large files. 
    * Ensure atomic operation (move + update metadata). 


### **Acceptance Criteria:**
  1. **Move to Folder** button is visible and functional. 
  2. Folder selection dropdown loads department folders correctly. 
  3. File moves successfully and is no longer in camera roll. 
  4. Permissions are enforced (only same department users can move). 
  5. No crashes or UI glitches during operation.

## US-24412 — Auto‑Flag and Restrict When Linked Shop DIN Is Revised or DCR Raised

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-24412 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Mandar Kamde |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\New Enhancement |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2025-12-15T06:04:51.517Z |
| Created Date | 2025-12-15T06:04:51.517Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/24412) |

### Description

As a **PLNG3** user, I want impacted **Index** rows to be clearly flagged and issuing restricted whenever any **linked Shop DIN** is revised/released or a **DCR** is created, so that I’m forced to review changes before proceeding.

### Acceptance Criteria

When a linked Shop DIN is **revised and released** or **any DCR** is created for it, the system **red‑highlights** the affected Index row, shows a **“Review Required”** hyperlink, and **restricts issuing**. The hyperlink opens a pop‑up with revised Shop DIN/DCR details and allows the user to decide **Revise** or **Keep as is** (mandatory comment). 
**Note:**
  * The BRD mentions visibility/ownership by **PMG** in some places and **PLNG3** in others. Reviewer role to be **confirmed** ; this story assumes **PLNG3**. 
  * Issuance restriction applies until **all impacted Index rows** in the same Planning DIN are reviewed. 


**Acceptance Criteria:**
  * **Trigger:** If any **linked Shop DIN** is revised/released **OR** any **DCR** is created for it, the associated Index row becomes **red‑highlighted**. 
  * A **“Review Required”** hyperlink (blue, underlined) appears on the impacted row; **tooltip:** “Linked Shop DIN revised. Click to review.” 
  * Impacted Index is **hidden** from non‑authorized users; hyperlink visible only to the **reviewer role** (PLNG3; role to be confirmed). 
  * **Restrictions:**
    * PLNG3 **cannot issue** any document in the Planning DIN until review is complete for all impacted rows. 
    * PLNG3 **cannot revise** a red‑highlighted row before completing the review. 
  * **Review Pop‑up:** Opens with **Shop DIN details** and **DCR info** ; actions: **Revise** or **Keep as is**. 
  * If **Keep as is** , a **mandatory comment** is required; if **Revise** , proceed to revision workflow. 
  * On decision, **red highlight** is **removed** ; status updates to **Revised** or **No Revision**. 
  * **Error Handling:** On failure to submit decision, show: “Unable to update status. Please retry.”

| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/24413) |

### Description

As a **PLNG3** reviewer, I want a **Verify Index Documents** window that lists impacted documents by project with clear statuses so that I can efficiently review and decide.

### Acceptance Criteria

Provide a dedicated **Verify Index Documents** window with **Pending** (default) and **All** tabs. Display a table with project and document details, revised Shop DIN and DCR references, current status, updated by, and remarks. Support **Review** , **Revise** , and **Keep as is** actions via a modal. 
**Note:**
  * Batch decisions are **not required** ; single‑row actions are sufficient. 
  * If **PMG** is the intended reviewer, swap role references accordingly. 


**Acceptance Criteria:**
  * **Header:** “Verify Index Documents”. 
  * **Tabs:** **Pending** (default) and **All**. 
  * **Pending** tab columns: **Project** , **Document** , **Index File Name** , **Revised Shop DIN** , **DCR** ; actions: **Review** , **Revise** , **Keep as is**. 
  * **All** tab adds **Status** and **Updated By** columns; **Remark** is displayed where applicable. 
  * **Review** opens a **modal** showing Shop DIN details and DCR info; **comment box** appears and is **mandatory** when **Keep as is** is selected. 
  * **Submit Decision** updates **Status** and **Remark** and **removes red highlight** for the row. 
  * **Cancel** closes the modal without changes. 
  * **Error States:** Inline error appears on failed submission and the row remains pending.

### Description

As a Laboratory User, I want the address in all printed chemical reports to be updated so that the reports reflect the correct and current location details.

### Acceptance Criteria

Update the address displayed in printed reports for all chemical report types (**Chemical GN** , **Chemical Overlay** , **IGC Reports (Practice E & A)**, **Corrosion Test Other** , and **PMI**) from the old address to the new address. 
**Old Address:**
    
    L&T HEAVY ENGINEERING 
    CALIBRATION & TESTING LABORATORY HAZIRA 
    MANUFACTURING COMPLEX,
    Surat-Hazira Road, PO Bhatha, Surat, Gujarat PIN 394 510, INDIA
    
    
**New Address:**
    
    L&T HEAVY ENGINEERING CALIBRATION & TESTING LABORATORY 
    A.M. NAIK HEAVY ENGINEERING COMPLEX, POST : Bhatha
    DIST: SURAT, GUJARAT – 394510

  * All printed reports (**Chemical GN** , **Chemical Overlay** , **IGC Reports** , **Corrosion Test Other** , **PMI**) display the **new address** exactly as specified. 
  * No occurrence of the old address remains in any printed report template. 
  * Address formatting matches the new specification (line breaks and punctuation as shown). 
  * Change applies to **newly generated reports only** ; historical reports remain unchanged. 
  * Verified across all print templates and PDF outputs.

## US-24486 — Create a page to manage API configuration details

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-24486 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Komal Chandolure |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\IEMQS-SAP-Integration |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2025-12-23T04:14:57.54Z |
| Created Date | 2025-12-23T04:14:57.54Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/24486) |

### Description

**As a** system administrator  
**I want to** create a new page with fields to store API configuration details  
**So that** I can manage and update API settings easily for integration purposes

### Acceptance Criteria

### **Fields with Data Types**
  1. **API URL** – _String_ (Mandatory) 
  2. **Method** – _String_ (Allowed values: GET, POST, PUT, DELETE) 
  3. **AuthType** – _String_ (Allowed values: API Key, Bearer Token, Basic Auth) 
  4. **Key** – _String_ (Visible if AuthType = API Key) 
  5. **Value** – _String_ (Visible if AuthType = API Key) 
  6. **Token** – _String_ (Visible if AuthType = Bearer Token) 
  7. **Username** – _String_ (Visible if AuthType = Basic Auth) 
  8. **Password** – _String (Encrypted)_ (Visible if AuthType = Basic Auth) 
  9. **RequestType** – _String_ (Allowed values: JSON, XML, Form-Data) 
  10. **IsActive** – _Boolean_ (Checkbox) 
  11. **ModifiedBy** – _String_ (Auto-populated with current user) 
  12. **ModifiedOn** – _DateTime_ (Auto-populated with current timestamp) 


* * *
### **Validation Rules**
  * **API URL** : Mandatory, must be a valid URL format 
  * **Method** : Mandatory, must be one of the allowed values 
  * **AuthType** : Mandatory, controls visibility of related fields 
  * **Password** : Must be stored securely (hashed/encrypted) 
  * **ModifiedOn** : System-generated, cannot be edited

## US-24509 — IR -  Quick Onboarding - Perform quick onboarding

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-24509 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | Workforce Management by MX Techies |
| Iteration Path | Workforce Management by MX Techies\Iteration 1 |
| Changed Date | 2025-12-24T07:21:54.033Z |
| Created Date | 2025-12-24T05:22:00.593Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/24509) |

### Description

As an Industrial Relation, I want to view the Quick Onboarding Page, so that I can initiate the Quick Onboarding Request for the employee

### Acceptance Criteria

Quick Onboarding Page  

 \- User need to fill all the Mandatory Fields (*).  

 \- User will be able to view these fields in the quick onboarding page,  

  

Name Information:  

 \- Title* 'dropdown' (fetched from title master)  

 \- Name as per Aadhaar / Full Name*  

 \- First Name*  

 \- Middle Name  

 \- Last Name*  

  

Biographical Information  

 \- Date Of Birth* 'Date picker'  

 \- Place Of Birth* - 'text field'  

  

Contact Information  

\- Email ID 'text field'  

\- Mobile Number 'text field'   

  

Personal Information  

 \- Gender*  'dropdown' (Male, Female)  

 \- Marital Status*  'Radio Button' (Single, Married)  

 \- Marital Status Since* -  Date picker   

\-  No of children - 'numeric field'  

 \- Nationality*  dropdown (Fetched from Nationality Master)  

 \- Domicile (State)* 'dropdown' (Fetched from State Master)  

 \- Religion* 'dropdown' (Fetched from Religion Master)  

 \- Caste Code* 'dropdown'   

 \- Blood Group* 'dropdown' (Fetched from Blood group Master)  

\- Father Name*  'text field'  

  

National ID Information   

\- Document Type* -  'Dropdown' (fetched from Document dropdown)  

\- Document ID* 'text field'  

\- Is Primary* 'Radio Button' (Yes, No)  

\- Upload document button   

\- Add icon (To add more document)  

\- Remove icon (to remove document)  

  

Organization Information  

 \- Dept Code*  \- (fetched from Dept code master)  

  

Job Information   

 \- Unit Name*  'dropdown' (from location Master)  

 \- Function Code  'dropdown' (From function code Master)  

 \- With Company?  'Radio Button' ('Yes', 'No')  

 \- Designation  

 \- Category Code* 'dropdown' (Master)  

 \- Grade/Training Year 'numeric field'   

 \- Previous PS Number 'text field'  

 \- Shift Code* 'dropdown' (From shit code Master)  

 \- Direct Workman?* 'Radio Button' (Yes, No)  

 \- Employee Type* 'dropdown' (From employee type master)  

 \- Ex-Trainee? 'dropdown' (Yes, No)  

 \- Area* 'dropdown' (From department code master)  

  

Compensation Information  

 \- Cadre at Joining* 'dropdown' (From cadre Master)  

 \- Current Cadre* 'dropdown' (From cadre Master)  

 \- Joining Basic* 'text field'     

 \- Confirmation Basic 'text field'  

 \- Current Basic* 'text field'  

  

Right Side Pane  

 \- 'Upload Image'  

  

  

 \- After filling  values,  user need to click the Submit button, so that request will be sent to the IR Approver for IR approval and system will display   

Message    

'Your quick onboarding page has been successfully submitted'.  

   

\- user need to click 'Okay' button in the popup message to redirect to the workforce information onboarding overview page.  

   

Validations:  

\- If the mandatory field is not filled & by clicking on the submit button, system trigger a pop-up notification/email alert to IR with missing field.  

\- In the Pop-up notification, message displays 'Mandatory fields missing, please fill all the details to proceed further'.  

 \- user need to click on the Cancel button, then user will navigate to the previous page.  

  

Validations:  

 \- By clicking submit button system to trigger “mail’ to all IR users.   

 \- Name: First Name and Last Name are mandatory. Middle Name is optional.  

 \-  Date of Birth (DOB): Date picker and manual entry in DD-MM-YYYY format and age will be auto calculated and displays in the age field.  

 \- Document ID: Based on the document type the document ID field will be mandatory. For example, if the document type is Aadhaar then Aadhaar number will be mandatory. The list of mandatory fields based on the document type will be fetched from the document master.  

 \- Aadhar “Masking” should be applied after filling the details, Masking of first 8 digits and only display last 4 digits.   

 \- Aadhar should be encrypted, and data should be securely stored in the background.   

 \- Document upload and Preview option provided  

 \- In the Married or Single 'Radio button'. If single is selected, hide all other fields/If married is selected, display "Marital status since", "No. of children" filed  

 \- No. of child: Expand and collapse field based on the No. of children selected - Max No of children will be 5.  

 \- Document Attachment: File upload, file size limits (standard size limit for file upload - 5MB & configurable and will be used based on Mendix standard).  

 \- user will be able to upload multiple document: display name of the document & upload icon for each document.  

 \- Department Code (alphanumerical field): Auto fetch and display corresponding department name from master data   

\- Based on the Supervisor Assignment: Autofill these fields based on the department code, such as  

o      Immediate Supervisor (IS)   

o Next Supervisor (NS)   

o Department Head (DH)   

o Contact Supervisor (CS) (if exists for that department, else the field will be blank)   

o Extension number (office telephone)  

o Working Area (fetched from Shop name master)  

 \- User can manually select a different Immediate Supervisor with editable text filed.  

 \- Based on IS selection system should auto populate these fields, such as  

o Next Supervisor (NS)   

o Department Head (DH)   

\- (Displayed only when Marital status is selected as Married else will be hidden)  

\- Aadhar verification is not required for the onboarding and rehiring process.

| Field | Value |

| Changed Date | 2025-12-24T07:18:33.633Z |
| Created Date | 2025-12-24T07:14:11.853Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/24517) |

### Description

As an Industrial Relation, I want to view all the request which is created so that I can able to view all the details of respective request.

### Acceptance Criteria

Upon logging in to the application the user will be navigated to the IR dashboard, from the dashboard the user can click on the hamburger menu to open the side navigation.  

   

\- from the side navigation page user need to select the 'Onboarding Overview' sub menu from the 'Onboarding' menu.  

   

'Onboarding Overview' page will be displayed with the respective tabs.  

\- All  

\- Quick Onboarding  

\- Rehiring  

\- Full Onboarding  

  

\- User need to click on the 'All' tab  

User can able to view the list of all the details of the requests in the grid.  

Data grid:  

 \- PS no   

 \- Name   

 \- Employment Type   

 \- Dept Code   

 \- Immediate Supervisor  

 \- Date of Joining   

 \- Submitted On  

 \- Approved On   

 \- Onboarding Type ('Quick Onboarding', 'Rehiring', 'Full   Onboarding')  

 \- Status  

  

 \- By clicking the PS no/Name (link) of any employee in the data grid, the user will be directed to the workforce information details page.  

  

Search Bar  

 \- The User should be able to search the respective request using the search bar.  

  

Page Navigation  

 \- The User should be able to navigate to other pages using the page navigation.  

  

Validations:  

 \- The PS no will be displayed based on the status, such as  

o Pending   

o Approved  

o Completed  

o Returned   

\- when the status is Pending or Retured, system will not display PS No in the PS no column.   

\- System will display PS No only when IR has approved the request and the status is displayed as "Approved" or "completed"   

  

 \- Submitted on: for quick onboarding and rehiring type - the date when the IR submitted the request for IR approver’s approval.  

 \- Submitted on: for full onboarding type - the date when the IR submitted the all the required fields for onboarding process of the employee will be displayed.

o Pending   

o Approved  

o Returned

## US-24549 — IR - Bulk Upload - View and select bulk upload option

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-24549 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | Workforce Management by MX Techies |
| Iteration Path | Workforce Management by MX Techies\Iteration 1 |
| Changed Date | 2025-12-29T05:47:55.307Z |
| Created Date | 2025-12-29T05:42:08.57Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/24549) |

### Description

As an Industrial Relation, I want to be able to view the Bulk Upload page so that I can upload the bulk upload document

### Acceptance Criteria

\- From the IR dashboard, the user need to select the side navigation page.
 \- from the side navigation page user need to select the 'Bulk Upload' sub menu from the 'Onboarding' menu.  

 \- 'Bulk Upload' page will be displayed with the following card option.  

   

List  of Cards:  

 \- Bulk Onboarding 'Upload File'  

 \- Bulk Update 'Upload File'  

 \- Bulk Image upload 
 \- Bulk Document upload 
  

Buttons:  

 \- Download Template  

  

User will be able to upload document (excel file) and perform the following action in bulk such as  

 \- Quick onboarding of workmen  

 \- Rehiring of workmen  

 \- Updating workmen detail  

 \- Separation  

  

User will be able to view a dropdown with the following options in the bulk onboarding option is selected,  

\- Quick onboarding   

\- Rehiring  

  

Based on the selection from the dropdown the list of entities and fields related to the selection will be displayed to the user,  

\- If user selects Quick onboarding, all the respective quick onboarding fields will be displayed to the user.  

\- If the user select rehiring all the entities related to full onboarding will be displayed.  

  

Validation:  

\- When user selects quick onboarding all the mandatory fields should be auto selected in the checkbox and user should not be able to change it.  

\- When the user selects Rehiring the respective mandatory fields should be auto selected in the checkbox and user should not be able to change it.   

\- Cadre   

\- Department  

\- Rejoining date  

\- Basic  

All the other fields apart from these mandatory fields will be displayed to the user and they will be able to select it based on requirement.  

  

 \- User can upload document (excel file) by drag & drop and by clicking on the upload button, user can upload file.  

  

Validations:  

 \-  Error message is displayed in popup in case the character of the fields is above max limit or invalid, or when mandatory field is missing in the excel file.  

 \- After validation the system will assign sequential PS numbers in the background to the employee.   

 \- Example: if the previous PS No ended at 125, and if IR bulk uploads 30 employee data, then the employee would receive PS numbers from 126 through 155 sequentially.  

 \- After upload, the system generates a downloadable Excel file that lists each employee’s name, assigned PS number, and any additional configured fields (configurable at runtime).

## US-24551 — IR - Bulk Upload - Bulk Image upload

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-24551 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | Workforce Management by MX Techies |
| Iteration Path | Workforce Management by MX Techies\Iteration 1 |
| Changed Date | 2025-12-29T11:17:03.933Z |
| Created Date | 2025-12-29T05:42:08.57Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/24551) |

### Description

As an Industrial Relation, I want to be able to fetch the Bulk image Upload, so that I store the images in the file directory

### Acceptance Criteria

List of Attributes: 
\- Upload 'button'    

  

\- when the user clicks on the upload button, user will be able to upload the URL of all the images.    

\- System should capture the URL and save all the data in the file directory.   

  

Buttons:  

 \- Save  

 \- Cancel  

   

 \- user need to click the save button to save the data.  

 \- user need to click on the Cancel button, then user will navigate to the previous page  

  

**Note:  
**
When a bulk photo upload is triggered, the system will search/fetch for photos in the specified location. Users should store all photos in that location, ensuring each photo is named with a **unique identifier** corresponding to the PS number. The system will then extract the PS number from the photo name and update the record by associating it with the respective photo. Example if PS number 123456 then name of photo file should be 123456.JPG.   

  

Photos download functionality will be there. Photos can download as per department, all photos or uploaded PS number through excel. Bulk photos should download in zip folder

### Description

As an Industrial Relation, I want to be able to perform the Bulk document Upload, so that I store the documents in the file directory

### Acceptance Criteria

List of Attributes: 
\- Upload document 'button'    

  

\- when the user clicks on the upload button, user will be able to upload the document, the document should contain URL link of the document along with the document type.    

\- System should capture the URL and save all the data in the file directory.

 \- Cancel

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-24555 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | Workforce Management by MX Techies |
| Iteration Path | Workforce Management by MX Techies\Iteration 1 |
| Changed Date | 2025-12-29T06:54:16.123Z |
| Created Date | 2025-12-29T06:13:11.503Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/24555) |

### Description

As an Industrial Relation, I should be able to add and/or edit the Event History details page so that I can save changes of respective Event History details.

### Acceptance Criteria

**Add event:**  
By clicking on the add button, 'add Event History' page will be displayed.  

\-  User need to fill all the mandatory fields (*).
  
**Edit event:**
By clicking on the edit button, 'Edit Event History' page will be displayed.  

\-  User need to fill all the mandatory fields (*).   

  

List  of Attributes:  

 \- Effective date (event start date)  

 \- Event 'dropdown' (fetched from events master)  

 \- From cadre 'drop down' (fetched from cadre master)  

 \- To cadre 'drop down' (fetched from cadre master)  

 \- Dept code 'drop down' (fetched from dept code master)  

 \- Function (auto filled based on dept code)  

 \- Basic 'text field'  

 \- Points 'text field'  

   

Buttons:  

 \- Save  

 \- Cancel  

  

 \- User need to click on Save button, the changes will be saved of respective Event history detail.  

 \- user need to click on the Cancel button, then user will navigate to the previous page.  
  

**Validations:  
**
 \- A new event will begin every time when an employee is onboarded. By default, the first event will be “Joining event” and it will be auto filled based on the quick onboarding details.  

 \- For apprentice, advance trainee and temporary workman only two events will be displayed (joining and separation) and once they become permanent workman, then a new PS no will be generated and then for this new PS number a new event will be generated as Joining event.

## US-24571 — IR Approver - Onboarding approval - View all request in All tab

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-24571 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | Workforce Management by MX Techies |
| Iteration Path | Workforce Management by MX Techies\Iteration 1 |
| Changed Date | 2025-12-29T10:48:15.57Z |
| Created Date | 2025-12-29T10:27:40.92Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/24571) |

### Description

As an Industrial Relation Approver, I want to view all the request so that I can view respective details by clicking on it.

### Acceptance Criteria

\- From the IR Approver dashboard, the user need to select the side navigation page.  

 \- In the side navigation page user need to select the 'Onboarding Approval' menu.  

 \- 'Onboarding Approval' page will be displayed with the following tabs.  

  

 The user need view the three tabs in the 'Onboarding Approval' such as 'All', 'Pending', 'Approved'.  

 \- User need to click on the 'All' tab  

User can able to view the list of all the requests in the grid.  

  

Data grid  

 \- All Select 'Checkbox'  

 \- PS no   

 \- Name   

 \- Employment type   

 \- Dept code   

 \- Immediate supervisor  

 \- Date of joining   

 \- Submitted on   

 \- Onboarding type  

 \- Status  

   

Button  

 \- Approve  

 \- Reject  

 \- Export  

  

 \- User can select all the list by clicking on “All select – checkbox”   

 \- User need to click on the approve button, pop-up message displays 'Data approved successful'.  

 \- user need to click Export button, when the data is exported pop up message displays 'Data Exported Successfully'.  

  

\- ‘PS No/Name’ hyperlink will be displayed in the grid view and by clicking on the respective request, user will be directed to the workmen details approval page.   

  

Search Bar  

 \- The User should be able to search the  respective request using the search bar.  

  

Page Navigation  

 \- The User should be able to navigate to other pages using the page navigation.  

  

Validations:  

 \- system will generate the PS number for quick onboarding and rehiring process only after approval from IR approver.   

 \- User can select multiple request and can approve in the bulk count  

 \- PS no: The PS no will be displayed based on these status such as 'Pending',  'Approved',  'Returned'.   

 \- Pending: waiting for the approval from the IR approver.  

 \- Approved: IR approver approved the onboarding process.   

 \- Returned: IR approver returned the onboarding or rehiring process.  

 \- Submitted on: The date when the IR submitted the quick onboarding or rehiring of the employee for IR approver’s approval.

## US-24574 — IR Approver - Onboarding approval - Approve/reject employee details in Employee overview page

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-24574 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | Workforce Management by MX Techies |
| Iteration Path | Workforce Management by MX Techies\Iteration 1 |
| Changed Date | 2025-12-29T11:07:59.217Z |
| Created Date | 2025-12-29T10:27:40.92Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/24574) |

### Description

As an Industrial Relation Approver, I want to click on the respective request so that I can view workmen details approval page and process the request.

### Acceptance Criteria

The user will be navigated to request view page upon clicking the PS no or Name in the following tab (all and pending).  

  

 \- User will be able to view all the details of the employee that was captured during the quick onboarding or rehiring will be auto populated in separate sections.   

 \- User will be able to approve the employee onboarding by clicking approve button.  

 \- A pop will be displayed when approval is successful with the message such as “Approval successful”  

 \- User will be able to reject the employee onboarding by clicking reject button.  

 \- A pop will be displayed with the message such as “Do you want to reject employee onboarding?”  

 \- When the user clicks Yes button in the pop up, the onboarding process will be returned, and the user will be redirected to the onboarding approval page.  

 \- When the user clicks No button in the pop up, the onboarding process will not be returned, and the user will stay in the workmen details approval page.

## US-24682 — SAP Outbound - Create Project and Deliverables

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-24682 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | TRISHUL TANDEL |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\IEMQS-SAP-Integration |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2026-01-21T05:53:23.62Z |
| Created Date | 2026-01-09T06:21:11.813Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/24682) |

### Description

**As a** SAP/CPI Integration Engineer  
**I want** the existing Project and Deliverables creation APIs to accept **multiple records in a single request**  
**So that** we can minimize the number of API calls and reduce **CPI token usage** , improving throughput and reliability. 
  

## In-Scope Endpoints (Existing) 
  1. **Project Creation**  
`POST https://phzpdiemqsweb.lthed.com/api/DESLN/AddProject`
  2. **Project Deliverables Creation**
     * `POST https://phzpdiemqsweb.lthed.com/api/DESLN/AddPart`
     * `POST https://phzpdiemqsweb.lthed.com/api/DESLN/AddBOM`


[](https://phzpdiemqsweb.lthed.com/api/DESLN/AddBOM)

### Acceptance Criteria

**Request Body** and **Response Body** shall have multiple as JSON array based on primary key and it's status.

## US-24718 — SAP Outbound - Item Purchase Data - Remove Dependency on COM013 Table

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-24718 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | TRISHUL TANDEL |
| Area Path | IEMQS 4.0\IEMQS-SAP-Integration |
| Iteration Path | IEMQS 4.0 |
| Changed Date | 2026-01-13T10:46:50.947Z |
| Created Date | 2026-01-13T10:07:47.437Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/24718) |

### Description

****
****
****
**As a** Data Integration Architect 
  
**I want** to remove the dependency on **COM013 (ERPLN table`ttdipu001175`)** for fetching consumable and flux item category 
  
**So that** IEMQS does not rely on obsolete data sources and ensures future-proof integration. 
  

### **Business Context**
  * COM013 was previously used to fetch **consumable and flux item category**. 
  * Last entries in these fields are **NULL** , indicating no active usage. 
  * For future releases, this table will not be referenced in IEMQS processes.

### Acceptance Criteria

* * **Remove COM013 References**
  * All IEMQS interfaces, APIs, and queries should no longer reference `ttdipu001175`. 


* **Update Data Model**
  * Remove any mapping or dependency on COM013 for item category. 


* **Validation**
  * Ensure no downstream process breaks due to removal. 


* **Documentation**
  * Update integration specs and architecture diagrams to reflect removal. 


* **Testing**
  * Validate that item category logic works without COM013 dependency. 


  

*

## US-25684 — IR -  Attendance - Maintain shift schedule

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-25684 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | Workforce Management by MX Techies |
| Iteration Path | Workforce Management by MX Techies |
| Changed Date | 2026-02-24T08:04:59.583Z |
| Created Date | 2026-02-24T07:54:58.563Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/25684) |

### Description

As an IR, I want to view the Maintain Shift Scheduling Plan details so that I can easily review the details.

### Acceptance Criteria

From the side navigation page user need to select the 'Maintain Shift Scheduling Plan' sub menu from the 'Attendance Management' menu.  

  

 \- 'Maintain Shift Scheduling Plan' page will be displayed.  

   

Attributes:  

 \- Shift Code* (shift code for every week fetched from shit code master)  

Based on the shift code selection, system should automatically display the from and to date of the week  

 \- From (DD-MM-YYYY)   

 \- To (DD-MM-YYYY)  

  

Grid Columns:  

 \- Employee (PS No and Employee Name) 'drop down' (fetched from workforce management)  

 \- Dept code and Shop Name (fetched from department code master)  

 \- Cadre (fetched from cadre master)   

 \- Category (fetched from category code master)   

 \- Shift (shift details will be fetched from the shift master)   

 \- Shift plan (fetched from shift rotation master)  

                     \- Monday  

                     \- Tuesday  

                     \- Wednesday  

                     \- Thursday  

                     \- Friday  

                     \- Saturday  

                     \- Sunday    

 \- Supervisor (will be auto populated based on dept. code)   

 \- TPT user (yes/no) (fetched from workforce info)  

 \- Last modified by (Displayed ad PS no, Name and Date) (the Shop person who modified the shift)   

 \- Action (edit icon button)   

 \- multi select check box for the user to select multiple employee details.   

  

Buttons:  

 \- Save  

 \- Export  

  

When the user clicks on the edit icon the following fields will be editable in the data grid,  

 \- Shift (shift details will be fetched from the shift master)   

 \- Shift plan (fetched from shift rotation master)  

                     \- Monday  

                     \- Tuesday  

                     \- Wednesday  

                     \- Thursday  

                     \- Friday  

                     \- Saturday  

                     \- Sunday   

  

Validation:  

\-  User will be able to change the shift for a selected date displayed on the table.   

\- When the user changes the "shift" column, then the shift details should be changed for the entire days displayed in the table. (eg., if the user changes the shift of an employee in the table from shift "II" to shift "I", the the shift plan for the all the days (Monday to Sunday, which ever applicable) should be changed automatically.  

\- When making the changes, system should make sure that the user is able to change the shifts only which are applicable to the user based on the shift rotation, all the other field entry should be restricted.     

  

Logics:  

\- System will automatically release the shift plan to the shop users based on the lock in period scheduled by the admin users.    

\-  Every week, as per the scheduled day and time, the shift plan will be released to the user. During this period the IR will not have any provision to edit the shift plans or schedule any pattern for any work man.   

\- IR will be able to make changes to the shift plan on any day apart from the Scheduled lock in period days.  

\-  User will be able to select multiple details in the data grid and change the scheduling patter for the selected users when clicking the "schedule shift" button.  

  

Search Bar:  

 \- The User should be able to search the respective request using the Column Search Bar and Global Search Bar.  

  

Page Navigation:  

 \- The User should be able to navigate to other pages using the page navigation.  

  

Validation:  

 \- The system should fetch the shift schedule plan from the shift rotation master and display the shift plan for each day in their respective columns.      

  

 \- If user click on the edit icon, user can able to edit the values such as in the respective row then the user need to click on the save button to save the changes in the existing page.  

  

 \- If user click on the save button, the edited changes need to changed and pop-up message need to be displayed  as 'Changes have been saved successfully'.

## US-25685 — IR -  Attendance - Maintain shift schedule

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-25685 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | Workforce Management by MX Techies |
| Iteration Path | Workforce Management by MX Techies |
| Changed Date | 2026-02-24T08:03:49.257Z |
| Created Date | 2026-02-24T07:54:58.563Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/25685) |

### Description

As an IR, I want to edit the Shift Scheduling Plan details so that I can review the details.

### Acceptance Criteria

When the user clicks on the "schedule shift" button, the following details will be displayed in a popup  

  

 \- List of employee (PS no and name) which was selected by the user before clicking the schedule shift button  

  

Fields:  

\- Shift pattern (fetched from shift rotation master and workforce info based on the shift type of the employee)  

\- Start date (date picker)   

\- End date (date picker)  

\- Forever  

\- Pattern week (drop down with the list of the pattern for the weeks in the current month, eg, week 1, week 2 , week 3, till week 5 based on te shift rotation pattern)  

  

Data grid:  

\- Rotation type (rotation shift weeks which was are displayed in the pattern week drop down will be displayed here).  

\- Monday to Sunday (display all the shift plan assigned for each day. Fetched from work force info and shift master)  

  

  

High points:  

\- Shift pattern: This is the rotational pattern of the shift (Eg., If a user has Shift pattern as I-III-II, this means that the user will be on the particular shift for a week like Shift I in week 1, in third shift in week 2 and second shift in week 3. This shift pattern will start when the user is onboarded - Based on the onboarding date) (Fetched from shift rotation master and workforce info)  

\- Start pattern on and Pattern week: This is the weeks of the pattern, In which week of the current months the shifts are patterned will be displayed (eg., If a month has 5 weeks and the current shift pattern is on week 3 to week  5, then system will display those three weeks in the drop down).  

  

The user will be able to save the shift plan by clicking the schedule shift button.   

The user will be able to revert the changes by clicking on the cancel button.  

  

\- Once the user selects any of the option and clicks on the schedule shift button, the following message should be displayed     

once the user saves the plan a message will be displayed as  

  

For option 1: “Shift plan successfully changed for one day”   

For option 2: “Shift plan successfully changed for current week only from DD-MM-YYYY to DD-MM-YYYY”   

For option 3: “New shift rotation will be carried forward for the workman from DD-MM-YYYY to "Forever"".   

  

  

Validation:  

\- the user will be able to change the shift plan by selecting the from date and to date.   

\- The user will be able to change the shift only for the selected week not for every week when they choose the start date and end date.  

\- the user will be able to change the shift for any number of days based on the start date and end date selection. System should not restrict the user to select the shift only for the given week, it can be for "n" number of days based on "Start date" and "End date".  

\- When the user selects the start date and clicks on "Forever" but, then system should change the shift patter for the employee from the start date and should follow the same shift pattern will the end of the employee's worktime in the organization.

As an Shop Head, Location Head, BU Head, I want to click the export button so that I can export all the details in the excel file.

### Acceptance Criteria

User need to click on the 'Export' button   
\- the list of details displayed in the respective tab will be exported to an excel file. \- If user selects from and to date, then the filtered value will be exported when the user click on the export button.   
 \- If user doesn't filter the data, then the existing value will be exported when the user click on the export button. User can save the export file to a location.

## US-25695 — User - Attendance - Maintain Regular timecard - date view

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| ID | US-25695 |
| Work Item Type | User Story |
| State | New |
| Priority | 2 |
| Assigned To | Unassigned |
| Area Path | Workforce Management by MX Techies |
| Iteration Path | Workforce Management by MX Techies |
| Changed Date | 2026-02-24T08:09:48.113Z |
| Created Date | 2026-02-24T08:09:48.113Z |
| Tags |  |
| ADO URL | [Open in ADO](https://dev.azure.com/LnT-HeavyEngineering-IEMQS/Workforce%20Management%20by%20MX%20Techies/_workitems/edit/25695) |

### Description

As a user, I want to view the Maintain Timecard details based on Date (Log-in and Log-out) so that I can easily review timecard entries for a specific day or date range.

### Acceptance Criteria

From the side navigation page user need to select the 'Maintain Timecard' sub menu from the 'Attendance Management' menu. \- 'Maintain Regular Timecard' page will be displayed with the following tabs such as 'Date View', 'Employee View'. \- By default, user will view the 'Date View' tab. Attributes: \- Date* 'date picker' Grid Columns: \- Employee (PS No and Employee Name) (fetched from info comm) \- TRT No (fetched from info comm - punching machine number) \- In (HH:MM) (fetched from info comm - punch in time) \- Out (HH:MM) (fetched from info comm - punch out time) \- Late (HH:MM) (fetched from info comm - time calculated after start time of the actual shift) \- Early (HH:MM) (fetched from info comm - time calculated before end time of the actual shift) \- Reg. Shift \- Actual Shift \- Employee status (Direct/indirect) (fetched from workforce information) \- Cadre (fetced from workforce information) \- Leave Remarks (fetched from "Leave remarks" master) Buttons: \- Save \- Export Search Bar: \- The User should be able to search the respective request using the Column search bar and Global Search Bar. Page Navigation: \- The User should be able to navigate to other pages using the page navigation. Validations: \- Default : In the grid view the user should able to view the punching data of the completed date and time (previous day and current day till shift 3 end). System should run a scheduler to fetch this data every morning around 9.30 am from the infocomm API. \- If user choose the 'Date' then the data related to that date will be displayed in the grid. If the user does not choose any date, then the data for the current date when the user is logged in should be displayed by default. \- In and out: Punch in and punch out time in Mins and Hours (Mins: 120 mins, Hours: 2:00) (eg. 484 � in mins , this is the time in mins. e.g. if in time is 08:04 then 8 * 60 min + 4 mins will be 484) \- Reg. Shift (Register): Planned shift � Fetched from "maintained shift schedule" module. It is the shift which is assigned for the workman every day. \- Act. Shift (Actual): punch in and out time of the employee calculated based on punching data. \- Timing of each shift, for auto scheduling will be captured from �Maintain shift timing master� in the backend. \- The system will consider only the first punch in and last punch out data and remove the duplicate punches and multiple punching data. \- The system will adjust the timing of the punching to the actual punch data in case of early punch in and late punch out. (ex: if workman punches in around 6:53 am for shift 1 and the shift start time is 7:00 am, then system will display the time as 6.53 am but will not pay any additional payment to the employee for that extra 7 mins, system will round off the extra time and will consider it as only 7:00 am, thus the employee will be only paid from 7:00 pm. Similarly for late punch out, if the shift time is 15:25 and the workman punches out at 15:35, then system should adjust the time to 15:25) \- If there is no Punch in and out data of the workman, then, system will consider it as absent (ABS). \- if remark is marked as ABS, it will remain ABS until the workman applies for PL or any other leave approval and get approved in the system. \- Based on the punch, leave remarks will be showed to the IR and to the employee as well (for employee it will be displayed in Kiosk). \- The system will run a schedule in real time basis and update the master card with the required punch data every day. Auto schedule will be done for yesterday's date and today's date, considering the night shift will end today.

### Description

**As a** user  
**I want** a new date-time field called **“Clearance Date and Time”** available for all RT stages  
**So that** I can capture the exact clearance timestamp consistently across the RT lifecycle.

### Acceptance Criteria

* A new field **“Clearance Date and Time”** is introduced for **all RT stages**. 
* The field supports **both date and time selection**. 
* The value is stored and retrievable without data loss. 
* Existing records remain unaffected unless updated.

### Description

**As a** user  
**I want** the same **“Clearance Date and Time”** functionality available in **Re-offer, Re-generate, and Return** stages  
**So that** clearance tracking remains consistent even during subsequent iterations.

### Acceptance Criteria

* “Clearance Date and Time” field is available in: 
  * Re-offer stage 
  * Re-generate stage 
  * Return stage 


* Field behavior (visibility, editability, validation) is **same as RT stages**. 
* Value can be updated independently for the second iteration. 
* No impact to first-iteration clearance data.
